9. [Staleness markers](#9-staleness-markers)
10. [Plan status](#10-plan-status)

## 1. System overview

TAQINOR OS is a multi-tenant ERP for a Moroccan solar installer. A browser loads a
**React/Vite single-page app** (`frontend/`). All traffic enters through **nginx**
(ports 80/443), which reverse-proxies three upstreams: the SPA static bundle, the
**Django REST API** (`backend/django_core`, served by gunicorn on :8000), and a
**FastAPI AI/OCR service** (`backend/fastapi_ia`, uvicorn on :8001). The SPA calls
the Django API under the prefix `/api/django/â€¦` and the AI service under
`/api/fastapi/â€¦`. Django persists everything to **PostgreSQL 16 (pgvector)** and
uses **Redis** as cache plus Celery broker; a **Celery worker** (same Django image)
runs async jobs such as quote-PDF generation. Generated PDFs and uploads live in
**MinIO** (S3-compatible object storage, buckets `erp-pdf` and `erp-uploads`).
Authentication is cookie-based JWT (httpOnly refresh cookie); every API request is
scoped to the caller's `company` (the tenant). The FastAPI service shares the same
Postgres for its OCR (Zhipu) and natural-language-SQL-agent (LangChain) features,
both JWT-protected and key-gated.

```
            â”Œâ”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”
  Browser â†’ â”‚    nginx     â”‚  :80 / :443  (+127.0.0.1:8090 lead webhook listener)
            â””â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”¬â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”˜
        â”Œâ”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”¼â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”¬â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”
        â–¼          â–¼               â–¼                   â–¼
   frontend   django_core      fastapi_ia          (static SPA)
   (Vite SPA) gunicorn :8000   uvicorn :8001
   /api/django/*               /api/fastapi/*
        â”‚          â”‚               â”‚
        â”‚          â–¼               â–¼
        â”‚     PostgreSQL 16 (pgvector)  â—„â”€â”€ shared DB
        â”‚          â”‚
        â”‚          â”œâ”€â”€ Redis  (cache + Celery broker)
        â”‚          â”œâ”€â”€ Celery worker (async PDFs, same Django image)
        â”‚          â””â”€â”€ MinIO  (erp-pdf, erp-uploads)
```

Request flow, front to back: SPA dispatches a Redux thunk â†’ axios `GET/POST
/api/django/<app>/â€¦` with the JWT cookie â†’ nginx â†’ gunicorn/Django â†’ DRF ViewSet
(queryset filtered to `request.user.company`) â†’ Postgres â†’ JSON back. Quote PDFs
are the exception: the ViewSet hands off to the vendored premium engine (sync via
`/proposal`, or async via Celery) which renders with WeasyPrint and stores the file
in MinIO.

## 4. Backend, app by app

All multi-tenant models carry a `company` FK â†’ `authentication.Company`. ViewSets
filter `get_queryset()` by `request.user.company` and force-assign `company` in
`perform_create` (never read from the request body). The literal tenant field is
**`company`** â€” there is no field named `tenant_id`.

> **Contrainte transversale de conception (VX125).** Budget de densitÃ© de
> signaux : max 3 signaux ambiants simultanÃ©s par Ã©cran de liste, jamais deux
> redisant le mÃªme chiffre ; badge de maturitÃ© `<BetaBadge>` sur les modules
> jeunes avec critÃ¨re de retrait objectif. Voir `docs/design-density-budget.md`.

API prefixes (from `erp_agentique/urls.py`, all under `/api/django/`):
`authentication` â†’ root, `stock/`, `crm/`, `ventes/`, `parametres/`, `roles/`,
`reporting/`, `contact/`, `installations/`, `sav/`, `records/`, `imports/`
(dataimport), `custom-fields/`, `documents/`, `public/` (tokenized PDFs, no login),
`pos/`, `scm/`, `mrp/`, `transport/`, `douane/`, `ecommerce-connect/`,
`promotions/` (this batch's 7 new apps + the pre-existing `pos`, all `_APP_URLS`).
JWT lives at `token/`, `token/refresh/`, `token/verify/`.

### authentication â€” tenant root, users, JWT  *(path: `backend/django_core/authentication`, NOT under apps/)*
Owns the tenant (`Company`), the user model, registration, and JWT issuance.
- **Company** â€” `nom`, `slug` (unique), `actif` (bool), `date_creation`; **NTDMO27** adds `tours_actifs` (bool, default True) â€” company-wide off-switch for auto-appearing `<ProductTour>` visits (any company, not demo-only, unlike `mode_presentation_actif`). The tenant every other model points at.
- **CustomUser** (extends AbstractUser) â€” `company` FKâ†’Company; `role` FKâ†’roles.Role (nullable); `role_legacy` (deprecated CharField admin/responsable/normal, now kept in sync with `role`'s tier on create/update + a one-off additive data backfill); derived `menu_tier` property = the **authoritative** menu tier read from the *new* Role (Administrateurâ†’admin, Responsableâ†’responsable, Utilisateur/customâ†’normal; superuserâ†’admin; legacy fallback only when role-less), exposed on `/auth/me/` and the JWT and used by the sidebar; `tier_for_role` + the pure `authentication/role_tiers.py` are the single source of truth; `poste`, `phone_number`, `avatar_key` (MinIO); `is_protected` (owner-account guard), `is_active`, `is_superuser`; **`supervisor`** self-FK (nullable, Feature E) driving team/subtree record-visibility. Record-visibility scoping lives in `authentication/scoping.py` (`record_scope_for`, `visible_user_ids`, `scope_queryset`) and is applied opt-in on the list+detail querysets of crm/ventes/installations/sav (only the new scoped roles narrow; admins/legacy/custom roles see all; users always keep their own records). Buy prices gated by `can_view_buy_prices` (`prix_achat_voir`).
- Endpoints (mounted at `/api/django/`): `POST /auth/register-company/` (public onboarding: new company + admin) Â· `POST /register/` (admin adds user to own company) Â· `GET /auth/me/` Â· `POST /auth/logout/` Â· `POST /auth/token/refresh/` Â· `GET/POST/PATCH/DELETE /users/â€¦` + `POST /users/{id}/avatar/` (Administrateur + Responsable tier â€” `IsAdminOrResponsableTier`, limited tier blocked) Â· `GET/POST/PATCH/DELETE /companies/â€¦` (superuser). **PACT116** gives `register-company` its first frontend door â€” public `/register` route (no login, no ERP layout), previously reachable only by direct API call.
- **Tenant lifecycle (SCA18-22).** `Company.statut` (actif/suspendu/fermeture, mirrors the historical `actif` bool both ways) gates login/JWT-refresh/API access â€” enforced in the authenticator (`cookie_auth.py`) and JWT issuance, never a middleware (SCA18, applied where the request already resolves the user â€” zero extra queries). `selectors.active_companies` (SCA19) is the single source of "sweepable" companies for beat fan-outs. `services.close_company`/soft-close (SCA21) moves a tenant to `fermeture` + timestamps `date_fermeture` (30-day grace before purge). A staff-only founder console (SCA22, `IsSuperuserConsole`) at `auth/console/tenants/` (list + usage counts via other apps' selectors, never their models) + `.../statut/` (change actif/suspendu/fermeture) + `.../note/` (free-text `plan_flag`, never billing). **SCA46** adds `Company.benchmarking_opt_in` (opt-in consent, default False) gating the k-anonymized cross-tenant aggregates (`core.benchmarking`). **SCA23** adds a "day-2" tenant test (`tests/test_day2_tenant.py`) as a CI sellability gate: registers a second company and exercises its full journey (quoteâ†’PDF proposalâ†’accept) in isolation from the first, catching cross-tenant regressions the single-company test suite would miss. **N100/N101 (2026-08-05, the ERP is now FOR SALE):** console gains `auth/console/tenants/creer/` (`views_console_create.py` â€” Company + CompanyProfile + first admin invitation + `init_roles` + SCA20 signup hooks, idempotent on slug/email) and the tenant payload gains plan/seats/health/licence-billing (import-guarded reads); `CustomUser.is_taqinor_support` (migration 0027) = editor-support flag granting only the right to REQUEST an impersonation (adminops NTADM22); public `POST /api/django/auth/signup-demande/` (design partners) is parked behind `TENANT_SIGNUP_ENABLED` (default OFF â†’ 404, throttled + honeypot) and only ever creates a `DemandeInscription` â€” accounts are created solely by founder approval in the console.

### crm â€” sales funnel + clients
Leads from creation through funnel stages, client records, Odoo-style chatter,
duplicate detection/merge, reversible archive.
- **Client** â€” `company` FK; `type_client` (PARTICULIER/ENTREPRISE); `nom/prenom`, `email` (optional), `telephone`, `adresse`; Moroccan IDs `cin/ice/if_fiscal/rc`; `custom_data` JSON. Unique `(company, email)` when email set. **NTCRM14** adds `derniere_alerte_dormance` (timestamp, anti-spam guard on the dormant-account nudge).
- **Lead** â€” `company` FK; `client` FKâ†’Client (nullable); `owner` FKâ†’CustomUser; `stage` (**STAGES.py keys**: NEW/CONTACTED/QUOTE_SENT/FOLLOW_UP/SIGNED/COLD, default NEW); `perdu` (bool lost-flag) + `motif_perte`; `canal` (META_ADS/WHATSAPP_CTWA/SITE_WEB/REFERENCE/TELEPHONE/WALK_IN/AUTRE); `priorite`, `tags`, `relance_date`; `type_installation` (RESIDENTIEL/COMMERCIAL/INDUSTRIEL/AGRICOLE); energy profile (`facture_hiver/ete`, `ete_differente` bool, `regularisation_8221` bool); **L4** (21/08/2026, + founder extension) phone-script fields: `occupation_jour` (PRESENT/ABSENT/PARTIEL, nullable â€” primes over the founder default in `courbes_journalieres._occupation` when set) and equipment `equip_piscine(_pompe_kw,_heures_jour)`/`equip_voiture_electrique`/`equip_ve_km_semaine(_chargeur_kw,_creneau)`/`equip_clim(_pieces,_kw)`/`equip_chauffe_eau_electrique(_kw,_creneau)` (migration crm/0079 adds the 6 power/crÃ©neau fields, crm/0080 adds `equip_clim_creneau`/`equip_piscine_creneau` â€” every one moves the hourly curve; the tunnel Â« Affiner mon profil Â» captures the kW/crÃ©neau details optionally per checked equipment and the webhook maps them with strict validation, never overwriting an existing value with emptiness; GPS rule (fondateur 24/08): `gps_lat`/`gps_lng` are NEVER overwritten by address-only updates â€” guards in the webhook anti-replay loop and `LeadSerializer.validate`, and the tunnel no longer requires the city when a valid pin is set; all nullable tri-state â€” `None` = not asked yet, distinct from `futures_charges`'s paramless web-questionnaire checklist; migration crm/0078; composed into `courbes_journalieres.equipements` â€” see below); roof/site + pump fields; `source` (OS_NATIVE/ODOO_IMPORT_TEST/SITE_WEB); `is_archived` (bool) + `archived_by/at`; `custom_data` JSON; `web_questionnaire`/`web_estimate` JSON (quote-journey v2: pro/agricole answers without a column + the exact numbers shown to the visitor, whitelisted server-side; migration crm/0062 â€” the webhook also maps 'professionnel' â†’ INDUSTRIEL and writes one automatic chatter note per created lead).
- **LeadActivity** â€” `lead` FK; `kind` (CREATION/MODIFICATION/NOTE); field-change log (`field/old_value/new_value`) or manual `body`; `user` FK; `bulk` bool.
- **QuestionnaireLien** (L-QUEST 25/08, migration crm/0081, TenantModel) â€” questionnaire Â« Ã  remplir chez soi Â» par lead : deux jetons (`token` client / `token_interne` aperÃ§u muet, POST refusÃ©), `expires_at` 30 j rouvrable (magic-link), `questions` JSON 3-Ã©tats, `sections_repondues`. **Whitelist ET ordre d'affichage `SECTIONS_CLES` (recalÃ© 25/08 â€” engagement croissant, donnÃ©es personnelles en dernier)** : occupation/equipements/energie/toiture/gps/photo_facture/photo_compteur/photo_tableau/**contact**. RÃ¨gles pures dans `apps/crm/questionnaire.py` (manquantes()/prefill()/appliquer_section() â€” rÃ©ponses mappÃ©es par le MÃŠME extracteur que le webhook site, jamais une seconde validation ; le vide n'Ã©crase jamais, GPS jamais Ã©crasÃ©) + **grain CHAMP `champs_a_poser()`/`sections_a_servir()` (ordre fondateur 25/08 Â« on ne redemande JAMAIS une donnÃ©e dÃ©jÃ  connue Â») : `_COUVERT_PAR` retire `adresse` des questions dÃ¨s que gps_lat+gps_lng sont posÃ©s (le repÃ¨re localise le toit au mÃ¨tre), une colonne connue revient prÃ©-remplie donc confirmable, et une section sans rien Ã  montrer n'ouvre pas d'Ã©cran mort**. Mint privÃ© `POST leads/{id}/questionnaire-lien/` (dÃ©faut = sections MANQUANTES, chatter Â« crÃ©Ã©/mis Ã  jour Â», jamais Â« envoyÃ© Â») ; public `GET/POST /api/django/crm/public/questionnaire/<token>/` (throttle, 404 gÃ©nÃ©rique, enregistrement PAR section ; le GET sert `champs` en plus de `prefill`). Page publique `apps/web /questionnaire/[token]` (mobile-first, **6 Ã©crans max au lieu de 9 sections â€” regroupement `ECRANS` dans `lib/questionnaire.ts` : les 3 photos sur UN Ã©cran, toiture+gps sur un autre ; la barre de progression compte les Ã‰CRANS**, reprise oÃ¹ on s'est arrÃªtÃ©, GPS gÃ©oloc, photos via intake, POST navigateur via proxy same-origin `/api/questionnaire-repondre` â€” un POST PAR SECTION, contrat inchangÃ©) ; dialogue ERP `QuestionnaireDialog` (workspace lead, patron L-SECT).
- **VisiteExterne** (L-TRACE 25/08, migrations crm/0082-0083 + notifications/0048, TenantModel) â€” traÃ§age anti-fraude de TOUS les points publics (visite_site/tunnel_lead/proposition/questionnaire/booking) : `appareil_id` PRIMAIRE (uuid localStorage â€” la MAC n'est pas collectable par le web ; IP = signal secondaire, partagÃ©e au Maroc), ip/user_agent/langue/duree_s, heartbeat regroupÃ©, `Lead.appareil_id` (crm/0082). Service `apps/crm/visites.py` (best-effort, jamais un point public cassÃ©) ; beacon public `POST public/visite/` AUTH = le MÃŠME secret que le webhook lead (X-Webhook-Secret, importÃ© littÃ©ralement de webhooks.py) via proxy worker `/api/visite` ; balise site `apps/web lib/visite.ts` (consentement WB29, jamais en aperÃ§u interne). Notifications enrichies (historique de visites Ã  la crÃ©ation de lead, IP/appareil Ã  l'ouverture de proposition), direction (rÃ´les Directeur/Administrateur) TOUJOURS destinataire, alertes ROUGES (sÃ©vÃ©ritÃ© `critique`) : re-demande de devis d'un appareil dÃ©jÃ  rattachÃ© Ã  un autre lead + corrÃ©lation concurrent (mÃªme appareil sur â‰¥2 leads sous 30 j).
- **LeadTag / Canal / MotifPerte** â€” per-company managed lists for tags, channels, loss reasons (each has `archived` bool; Canal has `protege`).
- **WebsiteLeadPayload** â€” raw webhook capture from taqinor.ma; `payload` JSON, `processed` bool, `lead` FK (never loses inbound data).
- **Parrainage** (referral program, N98) â€” `company` FK; `parrain` FKâ†’Client (the referrer); `filleul_lead` FKâ†’Lead and/or `filleul_client` FKâ†’Client (the referred) + free-text `filleul_nom`; `statut` (en_attente/converti/recompense_versee); `recompense` (Decimal, pre-filled from `parametres.CompanyProfile.referral_reward`); `notes`; `created_by`. Feature on/off via `CompanyProfile.referral_enabled`.
- Endpoints (`/api/django/crm/`): `clients/` and `leads/` ViewSets (CRUD) plus `leads/{id}/archiver|restaurer|whatsapp-devis|devis-auto|noter|merge|bulk`, `leads/{id}/duplicates`, `leads/doublons`, `leads/historique`, `leads/export-xlsx`, `clients/export-xlsx`; managed-list ViewSets `tags/`, `canaux/`, `motifs-perte/`; `parrainages/` (referrals); `assignable-users/`; `POST webhooks/website-leads/` (public, static secret).
- **management/import_odoo_leads** (N107) â€” `manage.py import_odoo_leads <path> --company <slug|id> [--dry-run]`: idempotent Odoo `crm.lead` importer reusing the `dataimport` parser (CSV/XLSX) + JSON; forces company server-side, reconciles on normalized email/phone + the existing `(company, external_system, external_id)` unique key (never duplicates), stage names from STAGES.py (unknown â†’ NEW). No-op without a file. The real 619-lead extraction stays manual/gated on the actual Odoo backup (PII, never committed). **FG242** adds **ConcurrentPerte** â€” on a LOST lead, captures the winning competitor + price/devise/motif (reuses the existing `Lead.perdu` flag, no hardcoded STAGES.py stage); acting user + company server-side, optional LeadActivity chatter note; ViewSet `concurrents-perte/` (`?lead=`). Migration crm/0029. **FG204** adds **PointContact** â€” a per-lead multi-touch attribution journal (canal reusing `Lead.Canal`, source, date, ordre, optional paid-channel cost) with a timeline + first/last-touch summary selector; endpoints `points-contact/` (+ `attribution/`) and a `leads/{id}/points-contact/` read action. Migration crm/0030.
- **ARC4** sweeps all 13 crm ViewSets onto `CompanyScopedModelViewSet` (behaviour identical, isolation now auto-covered by the generic tenant sweep test). **ARC18/56** add nullable `tiers` FKs mirroring `Client` (migration crm/0050) and `Lead` (migration crm/0051) into the `tiers.Tiers` directory (`tiers_bridge.py`). **ARC28/29/30/33** add `apps/crm/platform.py` (the first platform manifest, alongside `contrats`) declaring `searchable_models`/`record_targets`/`agent_actions_module` â€” `agent_actions.py` exposes read-only lead/client agent actions.
- **SiteProfile** (DC12, previously undocumented here) â€” a REUSABLE per-client energy/roof/pumping profile (OneToOneâ†’`Client`; taxonomies reuse `Lead`'s choices rather than redeclaring a divergent second list), saved once instead of re-entered on every devis. Endpoint `site-profiles/`; consumed read-only by `apps.ventes` via `selectors.site_profile_for_client` â€” see the ventes `prefill-site` action (**WIR99**), the action that finally gave this model a caller.
- **LB39** â€” the VX95 Â« Annuler Â» toast on a lead-stage change was PATCHing back to the previous stage and always hitting the funnel guard's 400 (a lead never regresses an Ã©tape) â€” the undo was dead in production. `LeadSerializer` now accepts a write-only, never-persisted `undo` boolean: `validate()` allows the regression ONLY when a server-side check (`_undo_of_last_stage_change`) confirms the PATCH is the EXACT inverse of this lead's most recent `stage` chatter entry, within a 300 s window â€” any other backward move stays refused. No model/migration change.
- **NTMIG26** adds a certification layer on **Partenaire** (apporteur d'affaires / sous-revendeur, historical table `compta_partenaire`): `niveau_certification` (aucun/enregistre/certifie/or/platine), `date_certification`, `date_expiration_certification`, `specialites` (JSON), `nb_deploiements_reussis` (denormalized mirror). `services.poser_compteur_deploiements` is the ONE write path for the counter â€” called from `apps.migration` (which owns the deployments table), never a direct `Partenaire.objects.update()` from another app. Migration crm/0069.
- **Dormance & engagement (NTCRM14/15/16/29)** â€” `management/detecter_comptes_dormants` + `selectors.comptes_dormants(company, seuil_jours=90)` flags signed `Client`s with no recent activity; `clients/dormants/` (list) + `clients/{id}/relancer-dormance/` (nudge, stamps `derniere_alerte_dormance`). `engagement.py` (NTCRM16) is a pure/stateless multi-signal engagement score (0-100: frÃ©quence contact/activitÃ© rÃ©cente/paiements Ã  temps/devis acceptÃ©s, Chaud/TiÃ¨de/Froid labels like `scoring.py`'s lead-scoring) at `clients/{id}/engagement/` + `clients/engagement-bulk/`. `selectors.portefeuille_commercial` (NTCRM29) â€” a commercial's owned accounts + risk flags, at `clients/mon-portefeuille/`. Frontend: `DormantAccountsWidget.jsx` + `dashboard/PortfolioWidget.jsx` on `CrmCockpit`.
- **SalleVente / SalleVenteItem / SalleVenteVue** (NTCRM17-19) â€” a tokenized public page bundling several devis/documents/links for ONE lead OR ONE client (never both/neither, `clean()`-enforced); security modelled on `ged.PartageGed` (unpredictable `token`, `expires_at` default 30 d, optional hashed `password_hash`, `actif` kill-switch). `SalleVenteItem` (devis/document/video_lien/note, `ordre`). `SalleVenteVue` logs each public visit (hashed IP only). `selectors.salle_vente_analytics`/`salle_vente_summary_for_lead`; **NTCRM27** `detecter_signal_interet_salle_vente` flags repeat views on a salle linked to an open lead and emits a `salle_vente_signal_interet` domain event (best-effort). Endpoints: `salles-vente/` CRUD (+ `items/`, `DELETE items/{item_id}/`, `analytics/`), `leads/{id}/salle-vente-analytics/`; public `GET /api/django/crm/salle-vente/<token>/` (frontend `/salle-vente/:token`, no login/layout). Frontend: `pages/crm/salle-vente/PublicSalleVentePage.jsx` (public) + `pages/crm/leads/SalleVenteAnalyticsBadge.jsx` (internal, on the lead workspace).
- **Apporteur / DealEnregistre** (NTCRM20-22) â€” a B2B deal-registration registry, deliberately SEPARATE from the pre-existing `Partenaire`/FG234 lead-submission portal (a concurrent-registration protection window has no equivalent there). `Apporteur` (nom/type/commission %/RIB/`token_acces` â€” read-only portal secret, no CustomUser account). `DealEnregistre` (`apporteur` FK, `lead` OneToOne, `statut` en_attente/approuve/rejete/expire/**a_payer** â€” NTCRM22 sets this + `montant_commission_du` on the linked devis's acceptance; `clean()` refuses a second apporteur registering the SAME prospect â€” email/phone/client identity match â€” while another's protection window, default 90 d, is active). Endpoints: `apporteurs/`, `deals-enregistres/` (+ `approuver/`, `rejeter/`, `a-payer/`); public read-only `GET /api/django/crm/apporteur-portail/<token>/mes-deals/` (token-only, never a session).
- **Defi** (NTCRM23/24/28) â€” a temporary, PUBLIC team gamification challenge (date window + leaderboard), distinct from the private/permanent individual `ObjectifCommercial` (FG39); reuses `ObjectifCommercial.Metric` strictly (no second metric taxonomy). `selectors.classement_defi` ranks users by the dÃ©fi's metric over its window. Endpoints: `defis/` CRUD (+ `classement/` NTCRM24 leaderboard, `export-xlsx/` NTCRM28), frontend `/crm/defis` (DefisPage, visible to the whole team, not just managers).
- **NTCRM26/30 â€” playbook + account-review gap closure.** `Playbook.condition` (JSON `core.rules` condition tree evaluated against `{type_installation, canal}`, empty = applies to every lead) + `selectors.playbooks_recommandes(lead, stage)` (similarity-recommended playbooks, consumed by the pre-existing `lead_playbook_view`). **NTCRM30** closes the backend test-coverage gap on `PlanCompte`/`RevueCompte` (structured account-review notes, scaffolded by NTCRM10/11 migration crm/0063, frontend already wired by PACT105 â€” previously undocumented here): a review posted to `revues-compte/` appears immediately, most-recent-first, in the account plan's timeline.

### ventes â€” quotes, orders, invoices, credit notes, payments, quote engine
The largest app: full quoteâ†’orderâ†’invoiceâ†’recovery lifecycle plus the vendored
premium quote-PDF engine.
- **Devis** (quote) â€” `company` FK; `reference` (per company+month); `client` FKâ†’crm.Client; `lead` FKâ†’crm.Lead (nullable, lead-primary quoting); `statut` (**brouillon/envoye/accepte/refuse/expire**); `mode_installation` (residentiel/industriel/agricole); `option_acceptee` (sans_batterie/avec_batterie); `etude_params` JSON (kWc, production, autoconso, payback, pump CV/HMT/dÃ©bitâ€¦); `taux_tva`, `remise_globale`; versioning (`version`, `version_parent`, `superseded_by`, `is_active`); discount approval (`remise_approuvee`, `remise_approuvee_par`); `fichier_pdf` (MinIO key). **FG52** adds `devise` (ISO 4217, default MAD) + `taux_change` to **Devis** and **Facture** (and `parametres.CompanyProfile.devise_defaut`): on API create without an explicit devise, the company default is applied (fallback MAD); the premium PDF `fmt()` and UBL export (`dgi_export.py`/`utils/ubl.py`) emit the document currency. No base-currency conversion (currency is document-borne). **SCA47** adds `prix_par_kwc` (Total TTC Ã· kWc, DecimalField) â€” derived and FROZEN write-once at first save when a kWc + total exist (null for kWc-less/pompage devis, never recomputed after); internal generator/BI signal ONLY, same regime as `stock.Produit.prix_achat` â€” never on a PDF or client-facing output. **SCA39** adds `Meta.indexes` `(company, statut)` + `(company, date_creation)` on Devis and Facture (concurrent `AddIndex`, no blocking lock) â€” the money-path query subset of NTPLT20. **NTCPQ16** adds `variante_de`/`variante_tier` (self-FK + Ã©conomique/standard/premium tag â€” a tier variant is a full draft Devis, distinct from the size-variant `version_parent`/QJ15 and the sans/avec-batterie couple). **NTCPQ13** adds `devis_origine`/`numero_renouvellement` (self-FK to the renewal chain's root devis; the source devis is never touched â€” distinct from the T10 revision, which only applies to a not-yet-accepted devis). **NTCPQ11** adds `clauses_appliquees` (JSON snapshot of the applicable `cpq.ClauseCGV` list, frozen once at brouillonâ†’envoyÃ©, read-only by the quote_engine, never a new renderer).
- **LigneDevis** â€” `devis` FK, `produit` FKâ†’stock.Produit, `designation`, `quantite`, `prix_unitaire`, `remise`, `taux_tva` (nullable â†’ falls back to devis rate; 10% panels / 20% other). **NTCPQ18** adds an optional `lot` FKâ†’`LotDevis` (NULL = hors lot, historical behaviour unchanged). **L-2OPT (25/08/2026)** adds `variante` (`''` commune | `'sans'` | `'avec'`, migration 0102) : les deux options d'un devis rÃ©sidentiel peuvent porter des dimensionnements DIFFÃ‰RENTS (ex. 22 panneaux sans / 26 avec) â€” composition fusionnÃ©e `services.composition_deux_optimiseurs`/`fusionner_kits`, split PDF/aval variante-d'abord (`quote_engine/builder._repartir_options`, `utils/options.filter_lines_for_option`), payload public `dimensionnement_options`+`production_par_option` (contrat `contract_samples/dimensionnement_options.json`); lignes historiques (`''`) inchangÃ©es au bit prÃ¨s.
- **NTCPQ13/14/16/18/20 devis satellites** â€” `ConfigurationDevisSnapshot` (auto fine-grained pre-send config history on a brouillon devis, distinct from version revision), `LotDevis` (multi-site devis: groups `LigneDevis` for its own sub-total + a consolidated total; coexists with the QJ29 multi-villa `groupe_index` display grouping), `AvenantDevis` (amendment diff on an already-accepted devis â€” `lignes_ajoutees`/`lignes_retirees` JSON snapshots, never `prix_achat`; may re-trigger the NTCPQ7 approval matrix via `approbation_requise`). Endpoints: `devis/{id}/renouveler/` (NTCPQ13), `devis/{id}/lots/` (NTCPQ18), `devis/{id}/avenants/` (NTCPQ14), `devis/{id}/historique-configuration/` (NTCPQ20).
- **BonCommande** (client order) â€” `devis` OneToOneâ†’Devis (nullable), `client` FK; `statut` (**en_attente/confirme/livre/annule**); marking `livre` decrements stock.
- **Facture** (invoice) â€” `devis` FK (new Ã©chÃ©ancier path) **and/or** `bon_commande` OneToOne (legacy path); `client` FK; `type_facture` (**acompte/intermediaire/solde/complete**); `statut` (**brouillon/emise/payee/en_retard/annulee**); `pourcentage`, `libelle`, frozen `montant_ht/tva/ttc`; recovery (`prochaine_relance`, `exclu_relances`); computed `montant_paye`, `avoirs_total`, `montant_du` (= TTC âˆ’ paid âˆ’ credits); `fichier_pdf/ubl`. **ARC24** adds an optional `condition_paiement_ref` FKâ†’`parametres.ConditionPaiement` (source of the default `conditions_paiement` label; the TextField stays master/overridable).
- **LigneFacture** â€” same shape as LigneDevis (`facture` FK).
- **Paiement** â€” `facture` FK; `montant`, `date_paiement`, `mode` (especes/virement/cheque/carte/prelevement/autre). **SCA45** adds provider-agnostic `provider_ref` + `idempotency_key` (company-scoped uniqueness) so an external payment gateway integration can reconcile/replay-guard without a bespoke field per provider.
- **Avoir** (credit note) + **LigneAvoir** â€” `facture` FK (PROTECT), `client` FK; `statut` (emise/annulee); `motif`; frozen amounts; offsets the invoice's `montant_du`.
- **DevisActivity** â€” quote chatter (CREATION/MODIFICATION/NOTE), like LeadActivity.
- **FollowupLevel / RelanceLog** â€” recovery escalation tiers and per-invoice follow-up trace.
- **ShareLink** â€” public tokenized link (`token` unique, `devis`/`facture` FK, `expires_at`, 30-day) for WhatsApp PDF delivery without login; `niveau` standard|confiance (migration 0100), `otp_lecture` bool, and `sections` JSON (migration 0101 â€” per-link section toggles roof3d/sld/pdf/bankable/economies/jour_type/gammes, absent = served, chosen by the commercial in the fiche-lead Â« Envoyer au client Â» dialog). **L-INTPREV (25/08)** adds `token_interne` (migration 0103, 3-step unique-after-backfill) : second jeton d'APERÃ‡U INTERNE â€” mÃªme page proposition, ZÃ‰RO trace (ni compteur/first-view, ni chatter, ni avance de stage YLEAD10, ni notification, ni beacon d'engagement), OTP non exigÃ©, signature refusÃ©e (404 gÃ©nÃ©rique) ; mint `share-link` renvoie `token_interne`/`path_interne`, ligne dÃ©diÃ©e du dialogue DevisTab (le WhatsApp n'envoie JAMAIS l'interne).
- **ParametresGammes** â€” PVMRQ (fondateur 18/08) per-company singleton: `deux_gammes` toggle, renamable `nom_essentielle`/`nom_premium`, `marques` JSON (fixed slots Essentielle/Premium â†’ {role: marque}, roles = `ROLES_AUTO_COMPOSITION` mirroring frontend `PRODUCT_CATEGORIES`); read by `_pick_product` (pinned brand wins; missing brand â†’ None, never a substitute) and by the quote-generator auto-fill. Endpoint `GET/PATCH parametres-gammes/` (GET any authenticated company user, PATCH responsable/admin). Migration ventes/0097.
- **MandatPaiement** (XCTR22, previously undocumented here) â€” recurring payment mandate on a tokenized card (no card data ever enters the ERP); endpoint `mandats-paiement/`. **PACT43** gives it its first internal screen (`/ventes/mandats-paiement`, responsable/admin â€” list by statut + revoke).
- **RemiseEncaissement** + **LigneRemiseEncaissement** (XFSM19, previously undocumented here) â€” field cash/cheque deposit declarations (technicien declares, responsable clÃ´ture, gap never hidden) + PDF bordereau; endpoint `remises-encaissement/`. **PACT46** gives it its first screen (`/ventes/remises-encaissement`, declaration open to any role, clÃ´ture responsable/admin).

- **quote_engine/** â€” premium PDF engine. `builder.py` maps an OS Devis â†’ the generator data dict (only sell-side `prix_unitaire`; `prix_achat` excluded) and routes by market mode to one of three renderers: `residential/` (redesigned 3-page residential proposal), `agricole/` (4-page pompage-solaire proposal â€” cover/at-a-glance, Ã©tude+schÃ©ma+charts, Ã©quipement+prix+FDA+garanties, rentabilitÃ© solaire-vs-butane-vs-diesel+signature; modules `renderer/render/theme/cover/study/yield_page/economics_page/charts/schematic/economics/constants/sample_data`), and the legacy `generate_devis_premium.py` (one-page + industriel + fallback). `installations.py` = shared cover-hero photo library that picks the installation photo whose kWc is **nearest** the quote (agricole falls back to residential/industriel of similar power); photos in `assets/installations/<mode>-<kwc>.jpg`. `pricing.py`, `catalog.py`. Buy-price exclusion asserted by `apps/ventes/tests/test_quote_engine_formats.py`; agricole engine by `test_agricole_quote.py`. The old 3 704-line `test_quote_engine.py` was split (19/08/2026) into `_formats` (page counts/totaux/TVA), `_residential`, `_documents`, `_builder` + `_quote_engine_common.py` (shared fixtures); `test_quote_engine.py` remains as a compatibility shim re-exporting those fixtures.
- **solar_design.py** (FG246/247/249) â€” electrical-engineering helpers: `string_design` (distributes N panels across the inverter MPPT inputs, checks string Vmp/Voc at cold temperature vs the MPPT/voltage window, reports the DC/AC ratio), `match_inverter` (picks a compatible catalogue inverter, classification keywords aligned with `builder.py`, never a price-less product), `optimize_orientation` (tilt/azimuth sweep via the existing PVGIS client). Pure + fully tested (`tests/test_solar_design.py`); not yet surfaced in an endpoint. **FG255** adds `ev_charger_sizing` â€” EV charging-station (borne de recharge VE) sizing: line current (mono 230 V / tri 400 V âˆš3), dedicated breaker calibre, charge duration/window-fit, recommended standard borne (3.7/7.4/11/22 kW), and PV coupling (solar surplus feeds the borne first, reporting solar-covered kWh + the lifted autoconsommation rate). Pure math, input-freedom preserved, no model/endpoint/PDF change. **FG256** adds `battery_storage_sizing` â€” two objectives: MAX autoconsommation (store daytime surplus, capped by the re-dischargeable night load) vs BACKUP for N critical hours (usable kWh/kW from critical load Ã— hours); returns usable + nominal kWh (Ã· DoDÃ—âˆšround-trip), recommended pack and the binding objective. Pure math. **FG257** adds `simulate_bankable_yield` â€” financial-grade P50/P90: Performance Ratio = Î (1âˆ’loss) over temperature/soiling/wiring/inverter/mismatch/availability, P50 = baseÃ—PR, P90/P75 via the Gaussian lower-tail quantile (z=1.282/0.674) of an annual-variability Ïƒ (default 6 %); returns PR, loss breakdown, P50/P90/P75, optional specific yield. Pure math. **FG258** adds `hourly_self_consumption` â€” per-hour min(load, production) over an 8760-h (or 24-h) load curve â†’ real autoconsommation rate, coverage rate, injected surplus, grid import; typical residential/commercial load + PV profiles as fallbacks, and `load_curve_from_xlsx` (parses a column via the already-present openpyxl). Pure math, divide-by-zero guarded. **FG259** adds `net_metering_savings` â€” values the injected hourly surplus (from FG258) under Moroccan net-metering (loi 13-09): compensated only up to same-tranche import Ã— `compensation_ratio` at the tranche tariff (pointe/pleine/creuse), honours the `surplus_injecte_compense` toggle, optional annual cap + residual spill tariff. Pure math. **FG260** adds `tariff_escalation_projection` â€” a 20â€“25-year financial model: year-by-year escalated savings (ONEE escalation Ã— module degradation), cumulative + net, simple & discounted payback year, **NPV (VAN)** and **IRR (TRI)** solved by stdlib bisection/Newton (capped iterations, `None` on non-convergence). Pure math. **FG261** adds `optimize_subscribed_power` â€” for C&I clients: net grid demand (loadâˆ’PV) â†’ post-PV peak â†’ recommended subscribed power = ceil(peakÃ—margin) (never above current) + the annual capacity-charge saving, optional kWâ†’kVA via power factor. Pure math. **FG262** adds `module_degradation_curve` â€” per-year PV production factor (compound or linear, with a year-1 LID drop) confronted against manufacturer warranty floors (e.g. 90% @ yr10, 80% @ yr25), flagging the first breach year + shortfall. Pure math.
- **courbes_journalieres.py** (CJ1, 21/08/2026) â€” builds the public `courbes_journalieres` payload block (kept OUT of the money engine): per-season PVGIS production shapes in LOCAL Moroccan time (`apps/parametres/pvgis_profils.py` â€” 7 reference curves for 13 cities, live DRcalc/PVcalc at the lead's GPS with a 30-day system cache + outage circuit-breaker, honest omission when city unknown), real daily consumption levels from the 12 real bills, `options`/`batterie_kwh` mirroring the quote, `occupation` flag (residential default `presence_jour`, founder field decision). Consumed by the proposition Â« Sur une journÃ©e Â» chart (`apps/web/src/lib/dayProfiles.ts` â€” 3 sourced occupancy silhouettes, date-computed Ramadan window, kW units everywhere). **L4** (21/08/2026) adds an optional `equipements` sub-block (`_equipements`, via `apps.crm.selectors.equipements_pour_devis`, never `crm.models`): sourced hour-window + real-kW layers for `piscine`/`clim` (pure redistribution â€” `kwh_jour` unchanged, memo-cited BTUâ†’kWh conversion for clim) and `ve` (the one ADDITIVE layer â€” ADEME 19.8 kWh/100km Ã— real km/week, since EV charging is a future load absent from past bills); `chauffe_eau_electrique` stays a lead field only (no sourced layer). Key absent when no equipment data or no consumption block â€” byte-identical to pre-L4 output (pinned by `test_courbes_journalieres.py::EquipementsTests`). **L4 founder extension** â€” `_occupation` now consults `apps.crm.selectors.occupation_jour_pour_devis` (`Lead.occupation_jour`, phone script) FIRST: set â†’ served flag maps 1:1 (present/absent/partiel â†’ presence_jour/absence_jour/presence_partielle, source `lead_occupation_jour:<value>`, the first server-side path that can serve `presence_partielle` â€” previously visitor-only), unset â†’ unchanged historical default chain (pinned by `OccupationLeadTests`).
- **etude_horaire.py + quote_engine/bareme.py + dimensionnement.py + etude_horaire_view.py** (CJ2a, 21/08/2026) â€” **THE canonical residential savings engine**, replacing the `AUTOCONSO_SANS = 0.60` forfait (founder order: savings must follow the real consumption curves per season, not a flat 60 % of production). `bareme.py` = bidirectional kWhâ‡„MAD **calibrated on three REAL founder bills** (SRM Casablanca-Settat 08/05/2026, 20/12/2025, 20/01/2026): reproduces the 30-day bill to the centime (energy 496,03 / fixed 39,94 / TPPAN 56,80 / total 592,77 + 0,25 % cash stamp), proves the selective rule and the progressive day-prorated TPPAN (art. 16 dahir 1-96-77, VAT-inclusive), adds the TWO fixed lines (meter rental 18,28 HT + connection upkeep 15,00 HT), and inverts MADâ†’kWh by bisection on the TOTAL bill (exact on all three). Per-vintage tables with **per-tranche provenance**: T5 2026 corrected to 1,381704 (bill-proven; the repo extrapolated 1,405116 at constant HT) â€” `pricing.ONEE_TRANCHES` left UNTOUCHED so `test_tariff_drift_lock` stays green, divergence published in `DIVERGENCES_PRICING` (incl. the unresolved T6 2026 conflict). `etude_horaire.py` integrates hour by hour, month by month: real PVGIS production shape Ã— the client's occupancy silhouette + L4 equipment layers scaled to THAT month's real bill, overlap via the existing `solar_design.hourly_self_consumption`, battery by chronological daily charge/discharge (`BATTERY_ROUNDTRIP`, starts empty = conservative), money by the two-bill model AT THE MONTH. Returns `None` without real anchoring (rule Z2). `dimensionnement.py` = successor to the founder's Â« 900 DH/month Â» rule: sweeps candidate sizes (granularity = one catalogue panel; upper bound = production/consumption parity + 1 panel, since Moroccan surplus is worth nothing), reuses `composition_residentielle` for the cost AND the founder's 80 % inverter rule (never re-implemented), recommends by explicit named criterion (`meilleur_payback`, then coverage). `POST /ventes/etude-horaire/preview/` (`IsAnyRole`, company-scoped server-side, no writes) is the single source the generator screen will call (CJ2b) â€” contract `contract_samples/etude_horaire.json`. Consumed by `pricing.calculate_savings_roi(etude_horaire=â€¦)` (block absent â‡’ byte-identical to pre-CJ2a; the seller-entered C&I study still overrides), and the 12 monthly savings become twelve REAL computations instead of the hardcoded `_SF` split. `services.build_devis_auto` uses the hourly sizing only when `profil_reel_existe(lead)` (occupancy answered or an equipment declared WITH its magnitude) â€” otherwise the 900 DH rule is unchanged. The three occupancy silhouettes moved server-side into `courbes_journalieres.SILHOUETTES_OCCUPATION` (verbatim from `dayProfiles.ts` with their [A]/[S]/[i] provenance, pinned to the TS file by a source-pin test).
- **CJ2b (21/08/2026) â€” the hourly engine's numbers become VISIBLE, on all three surfaces.** (a) **Generator screen**: `frontend/src/features/ventes/etudeHorairePreviewPur.js` (pure, zero imports so `node --test` can run it) + `etudeHorairePreview.js` (the debounced/abortable hook) + `ventesApi.postEtudeHorairePreview` â€” `pages/ventes/DevisGenerator.jsx` now renders the sizing table (one row per candidate kWc, the 80 % inverter ratio visible, Â« Appliquer cette taille Â» replaying the existing `handleAutoFill`) and the per-season productionÃ—consumption overlap; the preview cards read the SERVER once it answers, `computeROI` staying the labelled offline fallback (untouched â€” its GHI table is pinned by `test_dc9_ghi_parity`). Battery figures are OMITTED with the engine's French reason whenever `batterie_disponible` is false for the quoted size. (b) **PDF**: `pricing._lire_etude_horaire` also returns `factures_avant_monthly` + `source_consommation`; `builder` accepts the hourly block as a REAL anchor (`factures_source` = `lead_12_mois` | `etude_horaire`, `factures_mensuelles_estimation`), which brings the whole economic layer â€” and the monthly chart â€” back to quotes that carry one real bill instead of twelve; `renderer.synthese_economies` publishes `eco_mensuelles` (the exact drawn gap) / `eco_mensuelles_total` / `eco_option`, and `charts.bill_before_after` writes the twelve MAD savings above the bars (legend inside the PNG â€” the card header is a flex row RENDERING_NOTES.md forbids growing; image height stays 33.5 mm so pagination cannot move). Z2 is unchanged for quotes with no anchor at all. (c) **Public page**: `public_views._economies_mensuelles_publiques` serves the additive `economies_mensuelles` key (12 MAD/month sans/avec, totals, `modele`, `estimation`, French source sentence), gated on the same `synthese_economies` that already carries Z2, with `avec`/`total_avec` null when the battery option is not really sellable; `courbes_journalieres` now also serves the base consumption `forme` per season + `consommation_forme_source`, so `apps/web` prefers the served silhouette and keeps `OCCUPANCY_SHAPES` only as fallback; the Â« Avec batterie Â» control moved to the head of the curve block as a larger `.btn-pill`. (d) **Freshness**: `services.rafraichir_etude_horaire_devis` (strict residential, kWc from the canonical `panneaux_et_watt_lu`, short-circuit at the engine's own `_HORAIRE_TOLERANCE_KWC`) is wired onto every server save path â€” `DevisViewSet.perform_update`, `replace-lines`, `sync-layout`, and the three `LigneDevisViewSet` operations â€” so the block stops going stale after the auto-quote.
- **webhooks.py WREF2** (21/08/2026) â€” the website lead webhook assigns a SERVER reference `NOM-N` at creation (last-name slug, accent-stripped, highest-used+1 per company on `all_objects`, savepoint+retries; anti-replay never reassigns; response carries `client_ref`); the browser-provisional `TQ-XXXX` the site displayed lives in `Lead.client_ref_provisoire` (migration crm/0077) â€” BOTH are indexed by the three searches (Leads list filter, DRF `search_fields`, reporting omnibox).

- **utils/references.py** â€” numbering = highest-used + 1 per company+month (savepoint + retry on races); never `count()+1`. **ARC6** relocates the algorithm to `core.numbering` (the foundation home ~15 apps import across app boundaries); this module is now a byte-identical re-export shim.
- **Sweep + performance (ARC4/5, SCA39-43/45/47/49).** **ARC5** sweeps all 21 ventes ViewSets onto `CompanyScopedModelViewSet` (unified scoping; the public `/proposal` + `ShareLink` endpoints stay explicitly excluded/documented, asserted by `test_arc5_proposal_public_access.py`). **SCA40** collapses the `par_commercial` reporting aggregate into one grouped query (ends an unbounded N+1). **SCA41** makes xlsx exports async past a row-count threshold (`ventes/export/status/` polling, 202 response, company-scoped MinIO key, pilot NTPLT29/30) â€” wired into **FactureList** (journal ventes / export comptable: on a 202 it polls `ventesApi.exportStatus(jobId)` then downloads). **SCA43** wires `core.request_cache` under the devis-list PDF-preview path (QPERF1 landing â€” the previously-skipped Devis query budget test now passes). **SCA49** freezes the Devis JSON contract (`etude_params` included) with a serializer-keys snapshot test (`docs/devis-json-contract.md`). **ARC28** adds `apps/ventes/platform.py` (searchable/record/import surfaces). **ARC49/53** â€” see Â§5 frontend for the DevisList/FactureList migration onto the shared DataTable engine (parity proven, PDF/status flows untouched; see `docs/parity/ARC49-devislist-parite.md` and `ARC53-facturelist-parite.md`).
- **dgi/** (N105, silent DGI capability) â€” `dgi_export.py` (`build_ubl_xml`, UBL 2.1 invoice via stdlib `xml.etree`, carries seller+client ICE, per-line VAT, totals; no buy price), `dgi_validator.py` (`validate_dgi_conformity` â†’ list of FR problem messages), `toggle.py` (`is_dgi_enabled(company)`). Armed only by `parametres.CompanyProfile.dgi_export_actif` (default **OFF**): the two facture actions `dgi-export`/`dgi-conformite` and the `dgi_export_facture` management command **404/refuse when OFF**, and the Facture model/serializer/lists are byte-identical (no field, badge, status or column added). Simpl-TVA transmission + certified e-signature remain out of scope (G14). **FG263** adds `solar_design.ppa_model(...)` â€” a PPA / third-party-investor financial model (pure math): applies FG262 degradation, computes investor revenue (production Ã— PPA tariff + escalation âˆ’ O&M; NPV/IRR/payback reusing FG260) and client savings (grid âˆ’ PPA tariff), both perspectives. No model; quote-PDF path untouched. **FG264** adds `solar_design.pumping_cycle_yield(...)` â€” pure-math daily/monthly pumped-water volume by operating cycle (flat mode = byte-parity with `solar.js`; profile mode integrates dÃ©bit hour-by-hour weighted by a normalized clearsky irradiation profile); curve-less pumps â†’ None. No model; quote-PDF path untouched.

### stock â€” catalogue, suppliers, inventory, procurement
Product catalogue, multi-supplier sourcing, stock movements/locations, supplier POs
and returns.
- **Produit** â€” `company` FK; `nom`, `sku` (unique per company); `prix_vente` (sell HT); **`prix_achat`** (buy price â€” internal/generator-only, **never client-facing**); `quantite_stock` (canonical), `seuil_alerte`; `categorie`/`fournisseur` FK; commercial sheet (`marque`, `description`, `garantie`, `garantie_mois`, `garantie_production_mois`); pump specs (`pompe_cv`, `hmt_m`, `pompe_kw`, `tension_v`, `courbe_pompe` JSON); `is_archived`; `custom_data` JSON. **ARC27** adds an optional `unite` FKâ†’`parametres.UniteMesure`, mirroring the existing free-text `unite_stock` (which stays master; backfilled via `backfill_unites_mesure`).
- **Protection du catalogue RÃ‰EL (vague 2, 2026-07-31).** `Fournisseur` gagne `is_archived` (migration `stock/0082`) et le cycle produit : `destroy` ARCHIVE au lieu de dÃ©truire quand des donnÃ©es le retiennent, `?show_archived=true`, `PATCH unarchive/`, `DELETE force-delete/` (409 si un tarif subsiste). `achats.PrixFournisseur.fournisseur` passe **CASCADE â†’ PROTECT** (`achats/0003`) : supprimer un fournisseur ne peut plus dÃ©truire les prix d'achat NÃ‰GOCIÃ‰S. `FournisseurAdmin`/`ProduitAdmin`/`CompanyAdmin`/`ClientAdmin` refusent la suppression depuis l'admin Django (4 verrous, message nommant le chemin supportÃ©) ; `ProduitAdminForm` refuse de VIDER un `prix_achat`/`courbe_pompe` dÃ©jÃ  saisi. `CategorieAdmin` reste dÃ©libÃ©rÃ©ment sans garde (SET_NULL : les lignes produit survivent).
- **Categorie / Fournisseur / Marque** â€” referentials (Marque/`archived`). **ARC18** adds a nullable `Fournisseur.tiers` FK (migration stock/0075, `tiers_bridge.py`) mirroring identity one-way into `tiers.Tiers`. **ARC24** adds an optional `Fournisseur.condition_paiement_ref` FKâ†’`parametres.ConditionPaiement` mirroring the existing `delai_paiement_jours`/`fin_de_mois`/`escompte_pct` fields (backfilled via `backfill_conditions_paiement`).
- N14 (reservation-aware availability): `ProduitSerializer` exposes computed `quantite_reservee`, `quantite_disponible` (= stock âˆ’ active reservations from `installations.StockReservation`) and a reservation-aware low-stock flag; the legacy `is_low_stock` and `compute_besoin_materiel` are preserved (a chantier's own reservation is not double-counted).
- **MouvementStock** â€” `produit` FK; `type_mouvement` (entree/sortie/transfert/ajustement); `quantite_avant/apres`; `created_by`; the audit trail for every quantity change.
- **EmplacementStock / StockEmplacement / TransfertStock** â€” stock locations, per-location quantities (principal derived), and transfers between them.
- **FicheTechnique** (PV5) â€” one-to-one datasheet on `Produit`, scoped by `type_fiche` (module/onduleur/batterie/autre); all fields optional so a legacy fiche stays valid. Read cross-app ONLY through `stock.selectors.specs_for_produit`, which returns the block of the `type_fiche` **flat** (not a dict of blocks). **L-DECH** (2026-08-24, migration `stock/0130`) adds the battery-path POWER limits the hourly engine needed: `bat_max_decharge_kw` (discharge **per pack** â€” summed Ã— line quantity by `ventes.etude_horaire.puissances_batterie_des_lignes`, never derived from the charge figure) and `ond_bat_max_charge_kw`/`ond_bat_max_decharge_kw` (the hybrid inverter's battery PORT, the second bottleneck: the served/absorbed power is `min(Î£ packs, Î£ ports)`, in both directions). Values are seeded from official datasheets by `seed_catalogue` (Dyness 100 A Ã— 51,2 V; Deye ports 120â€“350 A at the same 51,2 V, so both bounds are comparable); NULL means NOT PUBLISHED â€” the engine then applies the founder's conservative rule rather than a supposed power.
- **PrixFournisseur** â€” per-supplier `prix_achat` (internal) for cheapest-sourcing.
- **BonCommandeFournisseur** + **LigneBonCommandeFournisseur** â€” supplier purchase orders; `statut` (brouillon/envoye/recu/annule); receipt increments stock via MouvementStock.
- **RetourFournisseur** + **LigneRetourFournisseur** â€” supplier returns; `statut` (brouillon/valide/annule); validation decrements stock.
- **APX18 â€” photo produit.** `POST/DELETE /stock/produits/<id>/photo/` : la photo n'est PAS un `ImageField` (la garde plateforme ARC26 les interdit) mais un `records.Attachment` (MinIO, cible de registre `stock.produit`), `Produit.photo` n'etant que le pointeur vers la photo canonique â€” validation par octets magiques, plafond 10 Mo, stockage cloisonne par societe et telechargement same-origin herites. Donnee INTERNE : jamais dans un PDF ni aucune sortie client, jamais rendue a cote de `prix_achat`.
- **Groupe NTWMS â€” couche entrepÃ´t (`models_wms.py`).** Warehouse execution layered on the existing catalogue/movement models â€” never a parallel bin hierarchy: zone/allÃ©e/casier (`BinLocation`) stays owned by `installations` (FG319), referenced here by string-FK only. **VaguePicking** â€” multi-source picking wave (`reference` race-safe, `mode_liberation` manuel/auto_heure/auto_seuil), + **LignePicking** (produit/quantitÃ©/`bin`/`lot`, `ordre_parcours` = physical walk order). **UniteLogistique** â€” addressable colis/palette carrying a GS1 SSCC (mod-10 checksum), self-FK `parent` for paletteâ†’colis nesting, `sceller()` freezes contents; + **UniteLogistiqueLigne** (contenu + scan audit). **Quai** + **RendezVousTransporteur** â€” dock scheduling with a server-side overlap guard (`save()`, never just `clean()`) and a `code_checkin` (unpredictable, `secrets`) for the driver kiosk (no ERP login, NTWMS8). **ExpeditionTransporteur** â€” one shipment of one `UniteLogistique` via a carrier provider (aucun/Amana/DHL/Chronopost/autre; `aucun` = internal label, zero network call). **PlanComptageTournant** â€” cycle-counting frequency per ABC class, feeds targeted `InventaireSession`s (never a parallel inventory mechanism).

### pos â€” vente comptoir / point of sale (Groupe XPOS, new app)  *( `/api/django/pos/` )*
Counter sales of accessories, built entirely on the existing ventes/stock/compta write paths (never a bespoke ledger).
- **Models** â€” `VenteComptoir` + `LigneVenteComptoir` (XPOS1: a counter sale that, on `valider/`, becomes a real `ventes.Facture` + `Paiement`(s) + a stock movement via `ventes.services`/`stock.services` â€” never a direct model import; `uuid_client` dedups an OFFLINE-created sale replayed on reconnect, NTRET1; `montant_arrhes`/`marchandise_remise` + `EN_ATTENTE_SOLDE` statut = deposit-on-order for backordered/custom items, NTRET5), `SessionCaisse` (XPOS4: caisse open/close, backed by the EXISTING `compta.Caisse` via `compta.services` â€” no duplicate cash journal; `numero_rapport_z` race-safe one-shot legal Z-report number, NTRET2; `montant_tpe_compte`/`ecart_tpe` card-terminal reconciliation, XPOS18), `ShareLinkTicket` (XPOS3: tokenized public link to the ticket PDF, same pattern as `ventes.ShareLink`, kept local to `apps/pos`), `CommandeRetrait` + `LigneCommandeRetrait` (XPOS15: click-and-collect, Ã _preparerâ†’pretâ†’retire), `ConfigMaterielPOS` (XPOS18: ESC/POS printer config + TPE card-terminal reconciliation), `CodePinCaissier` (NTRET3: hashed quick-lock PIN per company+user â€” switch cashier on a shared till without a full re-login or losing the in-progress cart).
- Endpoints: `ventes/` (+ `{id}/lignes`, `{id}/valider`, `{id}/promotions`, `{id}/coupon`, `{id}/arrhes|solde-arrhes|remettre-marchandise` (NTRET5), `{id}/ticket-pdf|ticket-arrhes-pdf|ticket-escpos|ticket-share-link`, `encaisser-facture/`, `emettre-carte-cadeau/`, `{id}/payer-carte-cadeau`, `factures-recherche/`, `dashboard/`, `dashboard-export/`, `dashboard-retail/` + `dashboard-retail-export/` (NTRET16 â€” panier moyen/transformation/ventes-mÂ²/top produits, reads `parametres.BoutiquePos`)), `sessions/` (+ `{id}/cloturer`, `{id}/rapport-x` (NTRET2, in-progress read-only), `{id}/rapport-z|rapport-z-pdf` (official, one-shot numbered)), `retraits/` (+ `{id}/lignes`, `{id}/marquer-pret`, `{id}/remettre`), `config-materiel/`; `verifier-pin/`+`definir-pin/` (NTRET3, PIN lock); public `GET /api/django/public/pos/ticket/<token>/` (tokenized ticket PDF, no auth, same pattern as `ventes.ShareLink`). Promotions/coupons/gift cards are `apps.promotions` models consumed here (never duplicated) â€” see the `promotions` section below.

all 61 installations ViewSets onto `CompanyScopedModelViewSet` (behaviour
identical). **ARC28** adds `apps/installations/platform.py`.
