No model. **FG304** adds **SousTraitant** â€” a subcontractor registry (mÃ©tier/contact/ICE/RIB + `actif` archive flag, default True content-type-independent), DISTINCT from material suppliers; company+created_by server-side. ViewSet `sous-traitants/` (mÃ©tier/actif filters, search). Migration installations/0020. **FG305** adds **OrdreSousTraitance** â€” subcontractor work orders (FKâ†’SousTraitant FG304 + same-app chantier, race-safe `OST-` ref, prestation/montant/Ã©chÃ©ance, cycle brouillonâ†’Ã©misâ†’en_coursâ†’rÃ©ceptionnÃ©â†’clos with lifecycle actions). ViewSet `ordres-sous-traitance/`. Migration installations/0021. **SCA34** converts `OrdreSousTraitance` into the FIRST pilot of the `core.documents.DocumentMetier` kit â€” a mixed conversion (redeclares `company` byte-identical, gains `created_at`/`updated_at` additively alongside the historical `date_creation`/`date_modification`, widens `statut` 20â†’32 chars for the abstract field, same 5 choices/default); reference format (`OST-YYYYMM-NNNN`) and the 3 existing lifecycle actions are unchanged (non-regression tested). **SCA36** converts `DemandeAchat` (models_demande_achat.py) into the kit's THIRD pilot (`LigneDocumentMetier` without a totals mixin â€” a purchase requisition has no money total). **NTP2P40** adds `demandes-achat/<id>/importer-lignes-csv/` â€” bulk-add requisition lines from an uploaded CSV. **NTP2P41** adds `rfq/<id>/comparatif-pdf/` (print-friendly PDF of `selectors.rfq_comparatif`) and `rfq/<id>/export-xlsx/` (one tab per consulted supplier + a comparatif tab, `apps.records.xlsx.build_workbook`) alongside the FG311 RFQ multi-supplier comparatif. **NTMOB21** adds `views_meteo.py`/`weather.py` â€” `GET installations/meteo/` (site weather for planning). **NTP2P2** adds **RegleApprobationAchat** + **EtapeApprobationAchat** (`models_approbation_achat.py`, migration installations/0101) â€” a same-app sequential approval plan for `DemandeAchat`, opt-in per company: a rule matches by montant bracket + optional chantier/programme scope (most-specific wins) and can `autorise_depassement_budget` (NTP2P4); no matching rule = the historical direct approve/refuse behaviour, byte-identical. ViewSet `regles-approbation-achat/`; `demandes-achat/{id}/` gains `etapes-approbation/` (GET), `approuver-etape/`, `rejeter-etape/` â€” the final step flips the demande to `approuvee`, a rejection to `refusee`. `services.controler_budget_demande_achat` (called at `soumettre`) reaches `stock.selectors/services` (never `stock.models`) to check the NTP2P4 department budget before the workflow starts.

### outillage â€” durable field tools & kits (F1/F2)
Durable tooling (drills, ladders, metersâ€¦), tracked **strictly separate from the consumable stock catalogue** â€” never sellable, never consumed, never on a client-facing document.
- **Outillage** (F1) â€” `company` FK; `nom`; `categorie` (free text); `asset_tag`; `numero_serie`; `emplacement` FKâ†’stock.EmplacementStock (nullable; the tool's home location among the existing dÃ©pÃ´t/camionnette); `statut` (DISPONIBLE/EN_INTERVENTION/EN_REPARATION/PERDU); `date_achat`; `note`. Optional photo via the generic `records.Attachment` (`outillage.outillage` whitelisted in `records.ALLOWED_TARGETS`).
- **KitOutillage / KitOutillageItem** (F2) â€” named, reusable tooling kit templates editable in ParamÃ¨tres; each an ordered list of catalogue tools (`KitOutillageItem.outil` FKâ†’Outillage, `ordre`, `unique_together (kit, outil)`); `type_intervention` (TypeIntervention key) pre-selects a kit; `actif` toggle. Three defaults (pose structure / raccordement / mise en service) seeded on first list (idempotent), fully editable.
- Endpoints (`/api/django/outillage/`): `outils/` (read any role, write responsable/admin; filter `?statut=`/`?emplacement=`, search nom/asset_tag/numero_serie/categorie), `kits/` (seed-on-list, write admin), `kit-items/` (write admin, item company follows its kit). Frontend route `/outillage` (CHANTIERS menu) + ParamÃ¨tres â†’ Â« Kits d'outillage Â» tab.

### sav â€” after-sales: equipment registry, tickets, maintenance contracts
Tracks installed equipment + warranty clocks and the SAV ticket lifecycle.
- **Equipement** â€” `company` FK; `produit` FKâ†’stock.Produit; `installation` FKâ†’installations.Installation; `numero_serie`; `date_pose`; `date_fin_garantie(_production)` (computed from `date_pose` + product warranty); `statut` (EN_SERVICE/REMPLACE/HORS_SERVICE); `remplace_par_ticket` FKâ†’Ticket.
- **Ticket** (SAV) â€” `company` FK; `reference`; `client` FK; `installation` FK (nullable); `equipement` FK (nullable); `type` (CORRECTIF/PREVENTIF); `statut` (NOUVEAU/PLANIFIE/EN_COURS/RESOLU/CLOTURE); `priorite`; `sous_garantie` (OUI/NON/A_DETERMINER, computed from equipment warranty if linked); `cout` (internal, never client-facing); `annule` bool + `motif_annulation`.
- **TicketActivity** â€” ticket chatter. **ContratMaintenance** â€” preventive contracts (`periodicite`, `date_debut`, `derniere_visite`, `actif`, `duree_mois`, `date_renouvellement`).
- **PieceConsommee** (N46) â€” parts consumed on a SAV ticket: `company` FK; `ticket` FKâ†’Ticket; `produit` FKâ†’stock.Produit; `quantite`; `stock_decremente` (guards double stock moves). Shown on the intervention report by designation/marque/quantitÃ© only â€” never buy price or margin; recording it can decrement stock via `MouvementStock`.
- Endpoints (`/api/django/sav/`): `equipements/`, `tickets/` (+ `{id}/historique|noter|annuler|reactiver|rapport-pdf`), `contrats-maintenance/`. **NTMOB23** adds `equipements/<id>/partage-qr/` â€” a QR-shareable equipment record for field techs. **FG280** adds **AlarmeOnduleur** â€” inverter alarms/faults DISTINCT from the SAV ticket (code/gravitÃ© info-warning-critique/Ã©quipement, statut active/acquittee/resolue/escaladee), with `acquitter` (server-side user+date, idempotent) and `escalader` (links or opens a SAV ticket) actions; ViewSet `alarmes-onduleur/`. Migration sav/0011.
- **ARC4** sweeps all 15 sav ViewSets onto `CompanyScopedModelViewSet`. **ARC37** makes `sav` an emitter on the `core.events` bus â€” `services.emettre_changement_statut_ticket` fires `ticket_resolu`/`equipement_remplace` at the guarded transition site (never a second state machine). **ARC28/29/30/33** add `apps/sav/platform.py`.

### portail â€” client self-service portal (Groupe FG228-233, new app)  *( `/api/django/portail/` )*
The Odoo Portal equivalent: a client logs in to see their own devis/factures/chantiers/tickets. Originally built inside `compta`; **ODX12** moved the models here with `db_table = 'compta_<model>'` (`SeparateDatabaseAndState`, state-only â€” no data moved) + a re-export shim left in `apps/compta/models.py`. Links to `crm.Client`/`crm.Lead`/devis/factures/chantiers/tickets are STRING-FK (never a `models` import of ventes/crm/sav/installations). **WIR95** upgraded these from opaque `PositiveIntegerField` ids to REAL `ForeignKey`s string-referencing the target model (`'ventes.Devis'`, `'facturation.Facture'`, `'crm.Client'`, `'crm.Lead'`, `'installations.Installation'`, `'sav.Ticket'`) with `on_delete=SET_NULL, db_constraint=False` â€” same pattern as `pos.CommandeRetrait.devis`: no DB-level FK constraint (the 5 domains stay decoupled), but a deletion via the ORM now nulls the reference instead of leaving a silent orphan id; the `_id` attname stays usable everywhere the old field was read. Auth surface (portal tokens/accounts) preserved byte-identical â€” no access widened by the split.
- **Models** â€” `ComptePortailClient` (tokenized client access account, reuses the client's email â€” no 2nd identity copy per DC32), `AcceptationDevisPortail` (quote e-acceptance from the portal, `devis` FK), `PaiementFacturePortail` (self-service invoice payment record, `facture` FK), `DocumentClientPortail` (client-visible document refs, `client`/`lead` FK â€” **WIR94**: the upload is now ALSO deposited as a real `ged.Document` via `ged.services.deposit_document` (lazy cross-app call, never a `ged.models` import) and referenced by `document_ged`; the historical `fichier` FileField is kept for backward compat), `JalonChantierPortail` (site-progress milestones surfaced to the client), `DemandeTicketPortail` (SAV ticket requests raised from the portal).
- Endpoints (new prefix; the SAME ViewSets are also still served under `/api/django/compta/â€¦` for backward compatibility, incl. the public tokenized `portail/<token>/â€¦` views which stay on compta): `comptes-portail/`, `acceptations-devis-portail/`, `paiements-facture-portail/`, `documents-client-portail/`, `jalons-chantier-portail/`, `demandes-ticket-portail/`.

### reporting â€” dashboards, KPIs, insights, audit log  *(no models)*
Read-only aggregation across crm/ventes/installations/sav/stock, role-filtered.
- Endpoints (`/api/django/reporting/`): `dashboard/`, `search/`, `notifications/`, `calendar/` and `calendar/reschedule/` (agenda events + drag-reschedule), `pipeline/` (funnel value by STAGES, weighted forecast), `reports/sales|stock|service/` (+`?export=xlsx`), `insights/recurring-revenue|audit-log|job-costing|analytics|commissions/`, `archive/client/{id}/` and `archive/chantier/{id}/`. `job-costing` (margin via internal `prix_achat`) and `commissions` (sales commission per `CompanyProfile.commission_mode`) are admin-only.
- **RapportDefinition** (NTEXT10, `rapport_builder.py`, previously undocumented here) â€” a rejouable cross-tab report definition (source query + optional `pivot_spec`); endpoint `rapport-definitions/` (+ an execute action building rows via `PivotSpec`/`build_pivot`). This batch's **PACT146** fixes the execute response shape: without a `pivot_spec` the payload is now a LITERAL `{'rows': rows}` (the `pivot` key stays absent, not a dynamically-omitted one â€” `scripts/check_api_shapes.py` only sees literal dict keys) and gives it its first screen, `/reporting/rapport-builder`. **PACT144** gives the pre-existing `ai_governance` "rapport pÃ©riodique" feature (see below) its first screen, `/reporting/rapport-periode`. **NTEXT11** adds `rapport-definitions/{id}/export/?format=csv|xlsx` â€” a file export of the replayed definition, reusing the existing subscription-export infra, buy-price/margin columns stripped.

### parametres â€” company profile, business settings, WhatsApp templates
- **CompanyProfile** (one per company) â€” identity + Moroccan legal IDs (`ice`, `identifiant_fiscal`, `rc`, `patente`, `cnss`, `rib`); branding (`logo_key`, `signature_key`, `couleur_principale`); `responsable_defaut_leads` FK (default lead owner); quote-gen knobs (`payment_terms` JSON, `quote_validity_days`, `tva_standard/panneaux`, ROI constants `onee_tarif_kwh`/`productible_kwh_kwc`/`rendement_global`, `remise_max_pct`, `discount_approval_threshold`, `agricole_pump_hours`); `default_installer` FK (default technician for new chantiers, N66; NULL = creator is responsable); sales commission (`commission_mode` off/pct_devis/par_kwc + `commission_valeur`, sensitive/admin-only, N99); referral toggle (`referral_enabled` bool + `referral_reward`, N98); silent DGI export master switch (`dgi_export_actif` bool, **default OFF**, N105 â€” arms the ventes `dgi/` capability, invisible while off); `doc_prefixes`/`doc_numbering` JSON. **SCA27** adds `site_web` (migration 0055) â€” the tenant's own site URL, consumed by the quote-engine residential PDF footer/legal band instead of a hardcoded founder fallback (byte-identical repli when empty).
- **TauxTVA / ConditionPaiement / UniteMesure** (ARC23/24/27, `models_taxes.py`/`models_payment_terms.py`/`models_units.py`) â€” per-company referentials, each a `core.TenantModel` subclass (redeclares `company` for its own `related_name`, ARC1 motif): `TauxTVA` (code/libelle/taux, Moroccan rates 20/14/10/7/0, one `defaut=True`) feeds the default rate at devis/facture creation without rewriting an already-frozen document rate; `CompanyProfile.tva_standard` (existing knob, editable in ParamÃ¨tres) takes PRECEDENCE over the referential's seeded default. `ConditionPaiement` (libellÃ©/dÃ©lai jours/fin de mois/escompte %) sources the default `Facture.conditions_paiement` label and mirrors `stock.Fournisseur`'s existing numeric fields. `UniteMesure` (code/libellÃ©) mirrors `stock.Produit.unite_stock` (which stays master). All three purely additive/declarative â€” absent referential falls back to prior hardcoded behaviour; seeded at signup, backfillable via `backfill_conditions_paiement`/`backfill_unites_mesure` management commands.
- **MessageTemplate** â€” WhatsApp templates by `cle` (devis/facture/relance), `corps_fr` + `corps_darija`.
- **EmailTemplate** (FG17, in `models_email.py`) â€” editable e-mail templates by `cle` (devis/facture/relance/notification): `sujet` + `corps` with the same placeholder whitelist as WhatsApp (`{civilite}{nom}{reference}{lien}{n}`), `unique_together company+cle`. Helpers `EmailTemplate.get_template`/`render` (tolerant) for the future automation-email rewire (intentionally NOT wired yet). Endpoints `email-templates/` (CRUD) + `email-templates/effective/` (defaultsâŠ•overrides) + `email-templates/bulk/` (upsert), writes audited.
- **SettingsAuditLog** â€” who changed which setting field.
- **StatutConfig** (N58, in `models_statuses.py`) â€” per-company display overlay for chantier/SAV/bon-de-commande statuses: `domaine` + canonical `cle` + `libelle` + `ordre` + `actif` (`unique_together company+domaine+cle`). Display-only â€” canonical keys & state machines stay in their source models; defaults read live from `Installation.STATUT_ORDER`/`Ticket.STATUT_ORDER`/`BonCommande.Statut` (`statuses_defaults.py`), so output is byte-identical until edited.
- Endpoints (`/api/django/parametres/`): `GET /`, `PUT/PATCH /update/`, `POST /upload-logo|upload-signature/`, `DELETE /delete-logo|delete-signature/`, `GET+PUT/PATCH /messages/`, `GET /audit/`; `statuts/` ViewSet (N58) + `statuts/effective/?domaine=` (full ordered effective list) + `statuts/bulk/` (upsert a domaine). Reads `GET /` and `GET /messages/` are open to any role; every write/audit endpoint (incl. `statuts/` writes) is the Administrateur + Responsable tier (`IsAdminOrResponsableTier`), limited tier blocked.
- **NTEXT19** adds `gabarits.py`/`views_gabarits.py` â€” read-only custom document templates (`gabarits-document/`) + `gabarits-document/<code>/rendre/?cible_id=<id>` (renders the template as PDF for one target record). **NTEXT39** adds `gabarits-document/<code>/apercu/` â€” renders the same template with SAMPLE data (no real record touched), streamed as `<code>-apercu.pdf`.
- **NTEXT19** adds `gabarits.py`/`views_gabarits.py` â€” read-only custom document templates (`gabarits-document/`) + `gabarits-document/<code>/rendre/?cible_id=<id>` (renders the template as PDF for one target record).
- **ParametresPos / BoutiquePos** (NTRET8, `models_pos.py`) â€” ParamÃ¨tres â†’ Point de vente section (`PointDeVenteSection.jsx`, embedded in `ParametresEntreprise`, no dedicated route). `ParametresPos` (one per company) carries `taux_horaire_comptoir` (counter labour rate, distinct from `CompanyProfile.taux_horaire_sav`); the line-discount threshold and printer/TPE config are REUSED as-is from `CompanyProfile.discount_approval_threshold` and `pos.ConfigMaterielPOS` â€” never duplicated here. `BoutiquePos` marks an EXISTING `stock.EmplacementStock` (string-FK) as a physical point of sale, with its own receipt address/hours + `surface_m2` (NTRET16, feeds the POS retail dashboard's sales-per-mÂ² KPI). Endpoints: `GET /parametres/pos/` + `PUT /parametres/pos/update/`, `pos-boutiques/` ViewSet.
- **Licence tier (NTADM7/8, 2026-08-05)** â€” `CompanyProfile.plan` (nullable FKâ†’`adminops.PlanLicence`, NULL = full access) + `nb_sieges_max` (nullable = unlimited); `parametres/feature_flags.py::has_feature(company, module_key)` (never restrictive by default â€” foundation only, nav masking stays on FG391/ODX); `services_licence.py` records plan changes in `SettingsAuditLog` + fires the `plan.changed` webhook; seat counting = `authentication.services.sieges_utilises`.

### scm â€” supply chain planning (Groupe NTSCM, new app)  *( `/api/django/scm/` )*
Demand forecasting, ABC classification, stock policies, a consolidated reappro dashboard, and a monthly S&OP cycle â€” a PLANNING layer above `apps.stock`'s existing execution (basic reappro/RFQ/DemandeAchat/transfers/supplier scorecard); reads `apps.stock` only via its `selectors.py`/`services.py`, never a model import.
- **PrevisionDemande** â€” per produit/segment/period (`YYYY-MM`) demand forecast; `methode` (moyenne_mobile/tendance/saisonnier/manuel). **EvenementDemande** â€” a signed % demand impact (promo/chantier majeur/rupture fournisseur/saisonnalitÃ©) over a date window, applied by `services.generer_previsions`.
- **ClassificationABC** â€” Pareto A/B/C ranking, recalculated server-side only (`selectors.classifier_abc`) and persisted HERE (not on `stock.Produit`, cross-app boundary) as a OneToOne. **PolitiqueStock** â€” per-produit min/max/ROP/safety-stock policy; `service_level_pct` seeded from the ABC class (A=95/B=90/C=85) but stays editable; ROP/safety-stock derived fields written ONLY by `services.recalculer_politiques_stock`.
- **CyclePlanificationSOP** â€” monthly S&OP cycle, a SEQUENTIAL state machine (brouillonâ†’revue_demandeâ†’revue_offreâ†’revue_financeâ†’rÃ©union_rÃ©conciliationâ†’approuvÃ©â†’clos) advanced only via `services.avancer_statut_cycle` (no step-skipping) or reopened via `services.reouvrir_cycle` (admin-only, audited). **LigneDemandeSOP** â€” a FROZEN snapshot of consensus demand per produit, taken automatically on brouillonâ†’revue_demande. **LigneOffreSOP** â€” mirrored supply snapshot (stock disponible + capacitÃ© appro fournisseur) vs the frozen demand, surfacing the offreâˆ’demande gap.
- Endpoints (`/api/django/scm/`): `previsions-demande/` (+ `generer`), `evenements-demande/`, `classification-abc/` (+ `recalculer`), `politiques-stock/` (+ `recalculer`), `cycles-sop/` (+ `{id}/avancer-statut|reouvrir|historique|lignes-demande|ajuster-demande|calculer-offre|ecarts|impact-financier`), `tableau-bord-reappro/` (+ `creer-bcf`, groups lines by supplier into draft `stock.BonCommandeFournisseur`s via `stock.services`). Responsable/admin only. Frontend: `/scm/reappro` + `/scm/sop` (admin-only) â€” see Â§5.

### mrp â€” Production / MRP II (Groupe NTMFG, new app)  *( `/api/django/mrp/` )*
Generic manufacturing engine â€” work centers, routings, capacitated manufacturing orders, MRP net-requirements, finite-capacity scheduling, an MES shop-floor terminal, and standard/actual costing. Distinct and complementary to `installations`' existing lightweight kitting (`Kit`/`KitComposant`/`OrdreAssemblage`) â€” never rebuilt here; reads `installations`/`stock` only via `selectors.py`/`services.py` or string-FK.
- **PosteDeCharge** â€” work center (machine/ligne/manuel/sous-traitÃ©), daily capacity + an INTERNAL hourly cost (never client-facing, like `Produit.prix_achat`); optional `sous_traitant` FKâ†’`stock.Fournisseur`.
- **Gamme** + **OperationGamme** â€” a reusable routing (ordered poste+temps standard) for one produit, versioned; optional `kit_source` FKâ†’`stock.KitProduit` read-only for backflush at OF closure when no `kit_ordre_assemblage` link exists.
- **OrdreFabrication** (OF) â€” capacitated manufacturing order; EXTENDS (never duplicates) `installations.OrdreAssemblage` via an optional `kit_ordre_assemblage` link (when present, that model owns the stock movement). **OperationOF** + **PauseOperationOF** â€” instantiated-from-gamme operations with MES start/pause/resume/finish timestamps + scrap tracking. **ReservationOF** â€” internal component reservation bookkeeping (does NOT affect the cross-app `stock.services.available_quantity`, which stays driven by `installations` reservations only).
- **CoutStandard** â€” versioned, immutable-once-created standard cost (matiÃ¨re/main-d'Å“uvre/indirect %) per produit â€” strictly internal, never in a client document.
- Endpoints (`/api/django/mrp/`): `postes-charge/` (+ `{id}/oee`), `gammes/`, `operations-gamme/`, `ordres-fabrication/` (+ `{id}/confirmer|cloturer|annuler|dispo-composants`), `operations-of/` (+ `{id}/replanifier|demarrer|pauser|reprendre|terminer`), `couts-standard/` (+ `figer`), `mrp-run/`, `charge-postes/`, `analyse-couts/`, `oee-postes/`. Frontend: `/mrp/ordres-fabrication|gantt|terminal|oee|analyse-couts` â€” see Â§5.

### transport â€” transport orders (Groupe SUPPLY/NTLOG1-20, new app)  *( `/api/django/transport/` )*
EnlÃ¨vement/livraison/inter-site/import/export transport orders, from planning through delivery, with freight cost tracking and carrier disputes. Cross-app links (`ventes`/`stock`/`installations`/`flotte`) are ALL opaque integer string-FKs (`<app>_<model>_id`), resolved at read time via the target app's selectors or a function-local `apps.get_model` â€” never a static model import. History/attachments reuse the generic `records.Activity`/`records.Attachment` â€” no new `*Activity`/`FileField`.
- **OrdreTransport** â€” `numero` (race-safe `core.numbering`); `type_flux` (enlÃ¨vement_livraison/inter_site/import/export); its OWN `statut` machine (brouillon/planifiÃ©/en_cours/livrÃ©/annulÃ©, independent of STAGES.py); `mode_transport` (flotte_propre/affrÃ¨tement) + `mode_acheminement_physique` (route/mer/air, feeds the CO2 estimate). **LigneOrdreTransport** â€” merchandise (produit opaque ref, poids/volume/valeur dÃ©clarÃ©e). **EtapeTransport** â€” enlÃ¨vementâ†’transitâ†’livraison steps.
- **CoutFretReel** â€” actual freight cost lines (transport/assurance/manutention/dÃ©douanement), the read point a future landed-cost extension (FG316) can consume. **LitigeTransport** â€” an independent carrier-dispute state machine (ouvert/en_traitement/rÃ©solu/rejetÃ©), modeled on `litiges.Reclamation` without importing it; transitions log to the parent order's chatter. **ReserveReception** â€” a reception-time reserve (photos via `records.Attachment`) that automatically opens a `LitigeTransport`.
- **FacteurEmissionCO2** â€” per-company, per-mode (route/mer/air) kg-CO2-per-tonne-km factor, editable in ParamÃ¨tres.
- Endpoints (`/api/django/transport/`): `ordres-transport/` (+ `{id}/etapes|comparer-transporteurs|co2|livrer`), `lignes-transport/`, `etapes-transport/`, `couts-fret/`, `litiges-transport/` (+ `{id}/prendre-en-charge|resoudre|rejeter|reclamer-transporteur`), `reserves-reception/`, `facteurs-emission-co2/`. Frontend: `/transport/ordres` â€” see Â§5.

### douane â€” customs & import/export (Groupe NTLOG, new app)  *( `/api/django/douane/` )*
Currently EXPORT-only (NTLOG14): `DossierExport`/`PieceDossierExport`/`ParametresDouane`. **The import side does not exist here** â€” NTLOG10's original plan conflicts with the pre-existing `installations.DossierImport` (FG315, owned by the PLAN_SERVICE domain); NTLOG10 stays `[BLOCKED]` pending a reconciliation that must edit `apps/installations` (out of this lane's write scope), and NTLOG11/12/13/21/22/26/30 all depend on it and are BLOCKED with it. **Trap for a future session: `douane.DossierImport` does NOT exist â€” do not assume it.**
- **DossierExport** â€” `numero` (race-safe); `incoterm` (EXW/FOB/CFR/CIF/DAP/DDP); optional `devis`/`facture` FK (read links into `ventes`/`facturation`); ports embarquement/dÃ©barquement, `pays_destinataire`, `devise`, `valeur_marchandise_devise`; its OWN `statut` (Ã _prÃ©parer/DUM_dÃ©posÃ©e/en_dÃ©douanement/levÃ©/clÃ´turÃ©), independent of any import statut. **PieceDossierExport** â€” required/deposited document slots (facture_export/packing_list/certificat_origine/DUM_export/EUR.1), the file itself in `records.Attachment`.
- **ParametresDouane** â€” one per company (`for_company` singleton pattern), `regime_douanier_par_defaut`, `alerte_expiration_jours` (J-30/15/7 default), `mention_estimation_droits` (label reserved for NTLOG13/30, both BLOCKED â€” nothing consumes it yet).
- Endpoints (`/api/django/douane/`): `dossiers-export/` (+ `export`), `dossiers-export-pieces/`, `parametres-douane/`. Frontend: `/parametres/douane` (ParamÃ¨tres tab, responsable/admin) â€” see Â§5.

### ecommerce_connect â€” Shopify/WooCommerce connectors (Groupe NTRET18/19, new app)  *( `/api/django/ecommerce-connect/` )*
**[GATED: no API key/secret in .env]** â€” every sync degrades to a total no-op (zero network calls); this module stores NO secret in the database, only non-sensitive metadata. `produit_id`/`facture_id` reference `stock.Produit`/`ventes.Facture` by opaque integer, never a cross-app FK. No frontend screen this batch.
- **ConnexionEcommerce** â€” one per (company, plateforme shopify/woocommerce); `boutique_url`, `actif` toggle (does NOT bypass the missing-key no-op), last catalogue/orders sync timestamps.
- **ProduitSync** â€” ERPâ†”external product mapping; `vendable_en_ligne` opt-in lives HERE (not a new field on `stock.Produit`); sync statut (ok/erreur/en_attente).
- **CommandeSync** â€” a PAID external order processed into the ERP; idempotency key = (`connexion`, `external_order_id`) so an at-least-once webhook replay never creates a duplicate invoice.
- Endpoints (`/api/django/ecommerce-connect/`): `connexions/` (+ `{id}/sync-catalogue`), `produits-sync/`, `commandes/`; public HMAC-signed webhooks `shopify/webhook/commande/` and `woocommerce/webhook/commande/` (signature = authentication, no login).

### promotions â€” cart promotion engine (Groupe NTRET12/13/15, new app)  *( `/api/django/promotions/` )*
Configurable cart promotion rules, unique-code coupons, and gift cards â€” consumed by `apps.pos` at checkout (`services.evaluer_panier`, the ONLY entry point `pos.services` calls). The calculation itself lives in a pure, ORM-free `engine.py`; `services.py` is the ORMâ†”engine bridge. `categorie`/`produit` reference `apps.stock` by string-FK only. No frontend screen of its own â€” consumed inside the `pos` coquille (Â§5).
- **ReglexPromotion** â€” a configurable rule: `type_regle` (remise_%_produit/remise_montant_panier/n_pour_m/plage_horaire "happy hour"), `priorite` + `cumulable` (non-cumulable rules: only the most advantageous applicable one is kept), optional targeting (`categorie`/`produit`/`montant_min_panier`) and validity window/days/hours. The only model with a CRUD ViewSet â€” read open to any role (the till needs to know active rules), write responsable/admin.
- **CouponUnique** + **CouponUtilisation** (NTRET13) â€” a single-code coupon tied to ONE `ReglexPromotion`, `mode_limite` (unique_par_client/global), consumed via `services.consommer_coupon` (server-side, journaled â€” no standalone CRUD, only through `pos`'s `coupon` action). Distinct from `compta.CodePromotion` (generic marketing campaign code, no usage tracking).
- **CarteCadeau** (NTRET15) â€” a gift card, sold at the till (no stock line â€” not an inventoried product), usable as a partial/total payment method across multiple visits until exhausted; debited via `services.debiter_carte_cadeau` (`select_for_update`, never negative).
- Endpoints (`/api/django/promotions/`): `regles/` (+ `simuler`, a dry-run cart-promotion preview for the till, no sale created). Coupons/gift cards have no standalone CRUD â€” consumed through `pos`'s `{id}/coupon`, `emettre-carte-cadeau/`, `{id}/payer-carte-cadeau` actions.

### roles â€” RBAC  *( `/api/django/roles/` )*
- **Role** â€” `company` FK; `nom` (unique per company); `permissions` JSON (validated against canonical `ALL_PERMISSIONS`); `est_systeme` bool (system roles undeletable). Linked from `CustomUser.role`.
- 2026-06-18 (Feature D): `ALL_PERMISSIONS` expanded to a moduleÃ—action grid + governance codes (`*_export`, `crm/ventes/sav_reassign`, `technicien_assign`, `prix_achat_voir`, `journal_activite_voir`, scope markers `records_scope_equipe`/`records_scope_sous_arbre`). **NTCPQ36** adds 4 CPQ permission codes: `cpq_regles_gerer`, `cpq_prix_contractuels_gerer`, `cpq_marge_voir` (ELEVATED tier, same palier as `marge_voir`/`prix_achat_voir`), `cpq_approbation_approuver`. `CANONICAL_SYSTEM_ROLES` seeds **seven** roles per company â€” Directeur, Administrateur (=Admin), Commercial responsable, Commercial, Technicien responsable, Technicien, Viewer â€” plus the legacy Responsable/Utilisateur kept for existing accounts. Seeder: `init_roles` (also maps ownersâ†’Directeur, custom commercialâ†’Commercial; N103: self-heals a drifted same-named system role to `est_systeme=True`). `role_tiers.py` now derives the tier from the authoritative permission signal first (`roles_gerer`â†’admin, `users_voir`â†’responsable) with the name mapping as fallback â€” so a Directeur/Administrateur whose seeded row drifted to `est_systeme=False` still resolves to the admin tier and keeps access to `/users/` and `/roles/` (N103 regression fix), without widening Commercial/Technicien/Viewer.
- Endpoints: Role ViewSet (CRUD, open to the Administrateur + Responsable tier via `IsAdminOrResponsableTier` â€” limited tier blocked; delete blocked if system or in-use) + `permissions-disponibles/`.

### identity â€” security & access foundation (Groupe NTSEC, new app)  *( `/api/django/identity/` )*
Company-scoped security controls, all INERT by default (off-by-default `mode`/toggle, never blocks a request until explicitly configured).
- **Models** â€” `NetworkPolicy` + `IpAllowRule` (NTSEC11: per-company IP/CIDR allowlist, `mode` off/monitor/enforce, enforced by `middleware.NetworkPolicyMiddleware`), `TrustedDevice` (NTSEC14: "remember this device" â€” skips MFA on a non-expired/non-revoked trusted device only if the company opts in), `IdentityProvider` (NTSEC1: per-company SSO SAML/OIDC federation metadata), `ScimToken` + `ScimGroupMapping` (NTSEC5/6: SCIM 2.0 provisioning bearer token, hashed like `publicapi.ApiKey`; SCIM-groupâ†’`roles.Role` mapping via string-id, no `roles.models` import), `BreakGlassGrant` (NTSEC22: time-boxed audited emergency Administrateur elevation that bypasses `enforce_sso`, gated `apps.identity.selectors.is_break_glass_active`).
- Endpoints: `network-policies/`, `ip-allow-rules/`, `trusted-devices/`, `providers/`; SCIM 2.0 `scim/v2/<company_slug>/Users|Groups` (token-authenticated, machine-to-machine); `service-accounts/` (NTSEC24, model lives in `publicapi`, exposed here); `login-banner/` (public); `break-glass/` and `posture/` (NTSEC27 consolidated security posture) â€” both Directeur-only.

### accessreview â€” access governance (NTSEC19, new app)  *( `/api/django/accessreview/` )*
- **Models** â€” `AccessReviewCampaign` (periodic access-review campaign: pÃ©rimÃ¨tre all/role/module, dates, statut ouverte/â€¦), `AccessReviewItem` (one row per in-scope user, attested or revoked by a manager â€” revocation removes the role via `apps.roles.services`, never a direct `roles.models` import), `SodRule` (separation-of-duties: a conflicting permission pair, `severite` info/warning/critique â€” critique can BLOCK a role assignment that would create the cumul).
- Endpoints: `campaigns/` (+ `{id}/attester/`), `sod-rules/` (+ `violations/`, `seed-standard/`). All `company`-scoped (`TenantModel`).
- **WIR136** â€” mounting dedup: `identity`/`accessreview` were each mounted TWICE in `erp_agentique/urls.py` â€” `identity` once inside the shared `_APP_URLS` list (â†’ both `api/django/` and `api/v1/`) and AGAIN standalone at `api/django/identity/` (harmless duplicate); `accessreview` was mounted ONLY standalone at `api/django/`, so it never got an `api/v1/accessreview/` mirror like every other app. Both are now solely in `_APP_URLS` â€” same `api/django/â€¦` paths as before, `accessreview` gains its missing `api/v1/` mirror, `frais` (new this run) joins the same list.

### audit â€” activity log (audit trail)  *( `/api/django/audit/` )*
- **AuditLog** â€” company-scoped (server-forced, nullable for failed login); `user` FK (null=system) + `actor_username` snapshot; `action` (create/update/delete/status/login/logout/login_failed/pdf/email/whatsapp/export/accept/refuse); `content_type` + `object_id` + `object_repr` (link-back snapshot); `detail`; `timestamp` (UTC, bucketed in Africa/Casablanca at read time).
- Capture: `apps/audit/signals.py` (post_save/post_delete + status-change via pre_save cache) on the main business models, gated by `apps/audit/middleware.py` (records only inside a request â†’ no seed/migration noise); login/logout in `authentication/views.py`, failed login via `user_login_failed`; key actions (PDF/export/WhatsApp) via explicit `recorder.record` calls. Best-effort â€” never blocks the request.
- Endpoints (gated on `journal_activite_voir`, Directeur-only by default): `stats/` (hourly buckets for a day, per-day for week/month, Casablanca, filterable), `entries/` (paginated filterable list, newest first), `meta/` (filter-bar data).

### records â€” generic activities + attachments  *( `/api/django/records/` )*
ContentType-based, attachable to Lead/Client/Installation/Ticket.
- **ActivityType** â€” configurable types (Appel/Email/Relanceâ€¦), `delai_defaut_jours`.
- **Activity** â€” generic FK target; `activity_type` FK; `due_date`; `assigned_to` FK; `done` bool + `done_at/by`; `auto_relance` bool (auto-synced from `Lead.relance_date`).
- **Attachment** â€” generic FK target; `file_key` (MinIO); `phase` (avant/pendant/aprÃ¨s for field photos).
- Endpoints: `activity-types/`, `activities/` (+ `mine/`, `{id}/done/`), `attachments/` (+ `{id}/download`, `attachments-count/`).
- **ARC30** switches `ALLOWED_TARGETS` from a literal set to a lazy union â€” `core.platform`'s `record_targets` surface, computed on first use, never at import â€” with the 19 historical targets migrated into 8 new `platform.py` manifests (outillage/rh/qhse/kb/ged/flotte/gestion_projet/ao) plus crm/contrats/sav/stock/ventes/installations from ARC29; a drop-in replacement (in/iteration/len identical, non-regression tested). **ARC8/26/SCA4/37** guard logic (no new bespoke `*Activity` chatter, no new `FileField` outside `records.Attachment`/`ged.Document`, no hand-rolled `company` FK model, no hand-rolled "document mÃ©tier" outside the SCA30 kit) lives in `apps/records/platform_guards.py` â€” pure text/regex scanners against frozen baselines in `apps/records/platform_baselines/`, consumed by both `scripts/check_platform.py` (CI) and the Django test runner.

### customfields â€” admin-defined custom fields  *( `/api/django/custom-fields/` )*
- **CustomFieldDef** â€” `module` (LEAD/CLIENT/PRODUIT), `code` (slug), `type` (TEXT/NUMBER/DATE/CHOICE/BOOLEAN), `options` JSON, `obligatoire/visible_liste/actif`. Values live in each target model's `custom_data` JSON (no schema migration).
- **ARC14** adds a data-driven registry (`app_label`/`model` pairs, not a hardcoded module enum) with 2 pilots â€” `contrats.Contrat`, `flotte.Vehicule` (each own a `custom_data` JSON field, migrations contrats/0038 + flotte/0057). **ARC31** switches the registry's central loader to `register_from_platform_manifests()` (`CustomfieldsConfig.ready()`), reading the `customfield_models` surface off `core.platform` manifests instead of explicit `register()` calls in each app's `ready()` â€” the 8 native + 2 pilot models are unchanged, non-regression tested.
- **CustomObjectDef** + **CustomRecord** (XPLT16, previously undocumented here) â€” no-code custom business objects: an admin defines an object (`code`/`libelle`/`icone`) whose fields are ordinary `CustomFieldDef` rows under `module='custom:<code>'` (full reuse, no second field-definition engine); each row of data lives in `CustomRecord.data`. NTEXT2/NTEXT3 auto-generate the list/form views from the field schema. Endpoints `objects/`, `custom-objects/<code>/records/(<id>/)`, `custom-objects/<code>/vue-liste/`, `custom-objects/<code>/vue-formulaire/`. **PACT140** gives it its first screens: admin `/parametres/objets-personnalises` (define objects/fields, admin-only) + one generic `/objets/:code` records screen serving every defined object.
- Endpoints: `definitions/` ViewSet.
- **NTEXT24** adds `blueprint.py` â€” export/import of the platform CONFIG (custom objects/fields), idempotent by natural key (re-import never duplicates). **NTEXT34** adds `objets-catalogue/` (list installable custom-object templates) + `objets-catalogue/installer/<code>/` (install one into the tenant). **NTEXT38** adds `verrouille` bool (migration 0008) on `CustomFieldDef`/`CustomObjectDef` (admin lock against accidental edit/delete) + `verrouiller`/`deverrouiller` actions on `definitions/<pk>/` and `objects/<pk>/`; `audit_plateforme.py` reports it.

### tiers â€” cross-app party directory (ARC17, foundation app)  *( `/api/django/tiers/` )*
The Odoo `res.partner` equivalent: one identity record instead of the same name/coordinates/legal-IDs being re-entered in 5+ domain models. A FOUNDATION app (import-linter `tiers-is-a-base-layer` â€” domain apps depend on it, never the reverse); this run only extends the pre-existing `Tiers` model + `TiersViewSet` (`ARC17`) with bridges and read-side reconciliation.
- **Tiers** â€” `company` FK; identity fields (`nom`/`prenom`/`raison_sociale`, `telephone`/`whatsapp`/`email`/`adresse`/`ville`/GPS, Moroccan IDs `ice`/`rc`/`identifiant_fiscal`/`cin`/`rib`); role flags `is_client`/`is_fournisseur`/`is_partenaire`/`is_soustraitant`; `type_tiers`.
- **ARC18** adds additive nullable `tiers` FKs (string-FK `'tiers.Tiers'`) mirroring identity ONE-WAY from `crm.Client` and `stock.Fournisseur` (migrations crm/0050, stock/0075) into a `Tiers` row, via `apps/{crm,stock}/tiers_bridge.py` + `attacher_ou_creer_tiers` (dedup by company-scoped email/ICE, else create â€” never duplicates; also persists the role flags on the create path, a real bug fix landed mid-run). **ARC19** extends the same bridge to `compta.Partenaire` (migration compta/0108) and `rh.DossierEmploye` (migration rh/0082). **ARC56** adds the bridge to `crm.Lead` (migration crm/0051) â€” pre-conversion identity, so a lead already has a `Tiers` before it becomes a client. In every case the historical model stays the IDENTITY MASTER; `tiers` is a mirror only.
- **ARC20** adds read-only cross-referencing: `selectors.find_by_ice`/`find_by_email` (company-scoped lookups) and `selectors.find_duplicates` â€” a same-ICE/same-email cluster report (e.g. one party registered as both a Fournisseur and a Partenaire) surfaced at `GET tiers/doublons/` (admin-only, no fusion/write).
- **ARC21** is a DECISION, flag-gated OFF: `TIERS_SOURCE_ECRITURE` would make `Tiers` the write-source of identity instead of a mirror â€” documented in `docs/decisions/ARC21-tiers-source-ecriture.md`, not flipped in this run.
- `management/backfill_tiers` â€” idempotent one-off command materializing `Tiers` bridges for existing rows across the four bridged models.
- Endpoints (`/api/django/tiers/`): `tiers/` ViewSet (CRUD, company-scoped, no dedicated fine-grained permission â€” authenticated + scoped suffices) + `tiers/doublons/` (ARC20 report, admin-only).

### migration â€” reprise de donnÃ©es client (NTMIG)  *( `/api/django/migration/` )*
- **ProjetMigration / LotMigration / RapportReconciliation** (`TenantModel`) â€” un projet de reprise par client, dÃ©coupÃ© en lots (un lot = une entitÃ© : clients, produits, fournisseursâ€¦). Chargement en **remplissage-seul FORCÃ‰** (`ecraser=False` codÃ© en dur, aucun interrupteur exposÃ© par l'API â€” une cellule vide ne remplace jamais une valeur saisie), via `apps/dataimport`. Connecteur Odoo (`charger-odoo`) et chargement par systÃ¨me externe partagent le mÃªme pipeline.

- **NTMIG-playbooks.** **PlaybookInstance** (`TenantModel`) â€” instantanÃ© FIGÃ‰ des Ã©tapes d'un `kb.KbArticle` playbook au moment de l'instanciation (une Ã©dition ultÃ©rieure de l'article ne modifie pas une instance dÃ©jÃ  en cours). **DeploiementPartenaire** â€” trace un dÃ©ploiement rÃ©alisÃ© par un partenaire certifiÃ©, alimente `crm.Partenaire.nb_deploiements_reussis` via `crm.services.poser_compteur_deploiements` (jamais un `.update()` direct depuis cette app). `certification.py` (scoring de certification partenaire), `playbooks.py`, `manage.py seed_playbooks` (idempotent). ### dataimport â€” CSV/XLSX import  *(`ImportJob`/`ImportJobRow`, `/api/django/imports/`)*
- `POST dry-run/` (preview + column mapping), `POST commit/`, `GET export/{entity}/`. Targets: leads, clients, products.
- **Garde d'Ã©crasement (vague 2, 2026-07-31).** En mode `maj`/`upsert` l'import Ã‰CRASAIT les champs dÃ©jÃ  remplis sans confirmation. DÃ©sormais : `ecraser` dÃ©faut **False** (remplissage seul â€” un champ vide se remplit, un champ rempli est PRÃ‰SERVÃ‰) ; `dry-run/` renvoie `conflits` (par ligne : cible + `ecrasements[{champ, ancienne, nouvelle}]` + `remplissages`) donc l'utilisateur voit AVANT Ã©criture ce qu'un fichier pÃ©rimÃ© remplacerait ; l'exÃ©cution journalise la valeur PRÃ‰CÃ‰DENTE de chaque champ modifiÃ© (`ImportJobRow.modifications`/`refuses`, + une ligne `AuditLog` par enregistrement via la primitive existante). Migration `dataimport/0004` additive.
- **ARC13** adds `parsing.py` `iter_rows(file_bytes, filename)` â€” a generic CSV/XLSX row reader (encoding detection utf-8/latin-1, `,`/`;` separator, normalized headers) that `services.parse_rows` now delegates to (zero behaviour change for the 6 historical FIELD_MAPS targets) and that any tabular-file reader outside `dataimport` can reuse instead of hand-rolling `csv.reader`/`openpyxl`; 3 pilot call sites migrated (rh/contrats views). **ARC32** makes `TARGETS` a lazy union of `FIELD_MAPS âˆª declared_import_specs()` â€” the `import_specs` surface declared by `core.platform` manifests (6 owning apps: crm already declared, stock/sav/flotte/contrats/rh extended), bridged via `export_registry.declared_import_specs()`; a set-literal non-regression test asserts the 8 historical targets plus any newly-declared target.

### contact â€” public contact form  *(no models, `/api/django/contact/`)*
- `POST /` â€” landing-page contact form; **parked by default** (returns 404 unless `CONTACT_FORM_ENABLED=1`).

### monitoring â€” production supervision (N50/N51/N52)  *( `/api/django/monitoring/` )*
- Models: `MonitoringConfig` (per installed-system provider + credentials, enabled), `ProductionReading` (manual/auto yield), `UnderperformanceFlag`, per-company settings (threshold % + auto-ticket toggle, default OFF).
- Swappable provider interface (registry + `NoOpProvider` default + `FusionSolarProvider` skeleton that no-ops without credentials; no new dependency).
- `configs/` (+ `providers/`, `{id}/sync-now/`), `readings/` (list + manual entry), `settings/`. Under-performance auto-creates an idempotent SAV ticket when enabled.

### notifications â€” unified notification engine (N75)  *( `/api/django/notifications/` )*
- Models: `Notification` (company + recipient-scoped), `NotificationPreference` (per userÃ—event channel toggles in_app/whatsapp/email). Service `notify()` is best-effort, respects preferences, reuses existing channels (no-op when unconfigured).
- `notifications/` (+ `unread-count/`, `{id}/read/`, `read-all/`), `preferences/`. In-app bell in the header + `/parametres/notifications`.
- **ARC35** registers `EventType.CONTRAT_SIGNE` â€” consumes the `contrat_signe` seam on the `core.events` bus, notifying the contract's creator (fallback managers) when a contract becomes fully signed; `contrats/receivers.py` additionally logs an ARC8 chatter note + a GED deposit on the same transition (best-effort, isolated failures logged separately per hook). **ARC37** makes `sav` and `gestion_projet` bus EMITTERS (not just consumers): `sav.services.emettre_changement_statut_ticket` fires `ticket_resolu`/`equipement_remplace` (â†’ `EventType.SAV_TICKET_RESOLU`/`SAV_EQUIPEMENT_REMPLACE`), and a project statut change fires `projet_status_change` (â†’ `PROJET_STATUT_CHANGE`). **ARC38** repatriates `qhse`'s `incident_declared` onto the `core.events` bus (double emission preserved for the existing local publicapi consumer, documented choice) via `qhse/receivers.py`. **ARC39** closes 3 previously-invisible notification gaps: `MONITORING_RAPPORT` (the periodic O&M report send, `monitoring/report.py`, stays a direct `EmailMessage` to the client but now ALSO notifies internal managers via `notifications.services`) and `PAIE_RIB_DIVERGENCE` (the ARC25 RIB-mismatch check already called `notify_many(...)` with this key â€” it was simply never a registered `EventType`, so it silently dropped; now persisted, zero caller behaviour change) + `PAIE_RUN_PRET` (a payroll run reaching `validee` now notifies payroll managers it is ready for the transfer order/closure).

### automation â€” no-code rules engine (N72/N73)  *( `/api/django/automation/` )*
- Models: `AutomationRule` (trigger + action config), `AutomationRun` (every run logged), `AutomationApproval` (owner-tier approval step). Fires on the app's own `post_save` signals, best-effort (never breaks the originating save); opt-in.
- `rules/` (+ `{id}/toggle/`), `runs/`, `approvals/` (+ `approve/`, `reject/`). ParamÃ¨tres â†’ Â« Automatisations Â».
- **ARC34** adds a generic `RECORD_STATE_CHANGE` trigger type â€” a registry-driven `(model, field)` whitelist read from each app's `platform.py` `automation_state_fields` manifest entry (`record_state_change_targets()`), with French validation at rule-creation time (serializer) and conditions evaluated via `core/rules.py` (FG367, never a second evaluator). 2 pilots emit through their app's `services.py` (`contrats.changer_statut` wrapper, `sav.emettre_changement_statut_ticket` at the guarded transition site) â€” never a direct model-signal shortcut.
- **NTEXT6** adds `list_sources.py` â€” a CLOSED registry of list sources for a FOR_EACH loop step (never an arbitrary queryset). **NTEXT7** adds `AutomationScheduledStep` (a frozen context + resume rank + statut, `en_attente/traitee/echouee`) for a WAIT step, resumed by the `automation.process_due_automation_steps` beat task (every 5 min, `beat_tasks.py`). **NTEXT31** adds `AutomationRun.SIMULATION` statut + `rules/{id}/simuler/` â€” a dry-run (no side effect) evaluation of a rule.

### publicapi â€” public REST API + webhooks (N89)  *( `/api/public/` data, `/api/django/publicapi/` management )*
- Models: `ApiKey` (hashed, scoped), `Webhook`, `WebhookDelivery`. `Api-Key` auth + per-key DRF throttle; read-only company-scoped `leads/devis/factures/chantiers` (never buy prices); HMAC-SHA256-signed webhooks on lead.created / devis.accepted / chantier.completed / facture.paid (httpx, best-effort). ParamÃ¨tres â†’ Â« API & Webhooks Â». NTADM41/42 (2026-08-05): webhook events `plan.changed` + `sieges.quota_atteint` (never client data) and `GET /api/public/v1/licence/statut/` (scope `read:licence`, exactly plan_code/modules_inclus/sieges_max/sieges_utilises).

### agent â€” agentic action catalogue (Group R, AG1)  *( `/api/django/agent/` )*
- No DB model â€” actions are declared in code via `apps/agent/registry.py` (`AgentAction`: key/label/description/inputs-schema/endpoint/method/required_permission/riskâˆˆinternalÂ·outwardÂ·irreversible/confirm_summary). `GET actions/` returns the per-caller, company+permission-filtered catalogue (cross-tenant leakage tested). Domain apps register their actions in `ready()` (ventes/crm/stock/sav/installations `agent_actions.py`, AG4â€“AG9). Execution stays the JWT-relay pattern (Django re-checks permission+company); outward/irreversible actions go through the FastAPI proposeâ†’confirm protocol.

### chat â€” internal team messaging Â« Discuss Â» (Group S)  *( `/api/django/chat/` )*
- Models: `Conversation` (dm/channel), `ConversationMember` (role/last_read_at/is_muted), `Message` (text/voice/system/record kinds, soft-delete, pin, reply_to), `MessageAttachment` (image/file/voice + transcript fields), `MessageReaction`, `MessageMention`, + generic shared-record link. Company **and** membership scoped everywhere (non-member 403, cross-tenant 404; company forced server-side). Endpoints: conversations (list/create/archive/read/unread/search/mute/members/leave), messages (`list?conversation=`/create/edit/delete/upload/react/pin/unpin/attachments-download/share-record via selectors). Notifications reuse `notify()` (CHAT_MESSAGE/CHAT_MENTION, mute-aware). Voice memos transcribed by a Celery task â†’ FastAPI faster-whisper (S10/S11), flag `CHAT_TRANSCRIPTION_ENABLED`; v1 real-time is polling (WebSocket upgrade S21 is gated on provisioned infra). **NTMOB30** adds `views_transcription.py` â€” `POST chat/transcrire/` (multipart `file`), a SYNCHRONOUS generic wrapper around the same S10/S11 Whisper call (never a second client) for dictating a field note straight onto a lead/ticket chatter, no attachment stored; disabled â†’ `{enabled: False}`, frontend falls back to keyboard.

Endpoints `cles-repartition/`(+`valider`), `lignes-cle-repartition/`, `allocations/`(+`executer`/`reverser`), `allocations-recurrentes/`, `engagements/`(+`liquider`/`verifier-disponible`), `modeles-cloture/`(+`seed`), `taches-cloture-modele/`, `instances-cloture/`(+`instancier`), `taches-cloture/`(+`cocher`/`generer-od`), `accruals-cloture/`(+`poster`), `justifications-variation/`, `rapprochements-compte/`(+`ouvrir`/`recalculer`/`soumettre`/`valider`/`rejeter`), `lignes-justification-compte/`, `composants-immobilisation/`(+`plans`), `depreciations-immobilisation/`(+`poster`/`reprendre`), `mutations-immobilisation/`, `immobilisations-en-cours/`(+`mettre-en-service`), `lignes-immobilisation-en-cours/`, `contrats-revenu/`(+`allouer`), `obligations-performance/`(+`generer-echeancier`), `echeances-reconnaissance/`(+`reconnaitre`), `cycles-consolidation/{id}/`(`tableau-flux|variation-capitaux|annexes|comparatif|etapes-audit|simuler|export-liasse`), and `etats/execution-budgetaire|analyse-variation|anomalies-ecritures|cockpit-cloture|pret-a-cloturer|rapprochements-en-retard|registre-immobilisations|projection-dotations|positions-contrat-revenu/`.
- **WIR107** exposes the pre-existing `ModeleEcriture`/`AbonnementEcriture` recurring-entry engine (XACC8, previously only driven by the `generer_ecritures_recurrentes` command/NTFIN32 close task) over REST for the Â« Ã‰critures rÃ©currentes Â» screen: `modeles-ecriture/` (+ `generer`, materializes one brouillon Ã©criture), `lignes-modele-ecriture/` (`?modele=`), `abonnements-ecriture/` (+ `generer-dues`, replays the idempotent-per-period batch on demand). **WIR85** finally wires the XACC6 permanent-inventory auto-posting that had zero production caller: `stock.services.record_stock_movement` now emits a new `core.events.mouvement_stock_enregistre` signal on every `MouvementStock`, consumed here by `receivers._ecriture_pour_mouvement_stock` â†’ `poster_mouvement_stock` (entrÃ©e/sortie/rebut/signed ajustement post; transfert never does â€” internal move, no value change). Still double-gated OFF by default (`COMPTA_AUTO_ECRITURES`/WIR24 toggle **and** `PlanComptable.inventaire_permanent`) â€” without both opt-ins, unchanged (no Ã©criture).

### frais â€” expense notes & site allowances (ODX15, new app)  *( `/api/django/frais/` )*
State-only split of FG135/FG136/ZACC6/XACC27/XACC28 OUT of `apps.compta` (migration `frais/0001_odx15_frais_split.py`, `SeparateDatabaseAndState`, `database_operations=[]`): every model **freezes its `db_table` on the historical `compta_*` name** â€” no table renamed, no data moved, revert is a plain `git revert`. `apps.frais` never imports `apps.compta.models`; it reaches accounts/Ã©critures/trÃ©sorerie only via **string-FK** (`'compta.CompteComptable'`, `'compta.EcritureComptable'`, `'compta.CompteTresorerie'`) and calls `apps.compta.services` for every posting + the FG115 period lock. A re-export shim in `apps/compta/models.py` (`from apps.frais.models import ...`) keeps existing importers working. **Deliberately distinct from `rh.NoteDeFrais`** (an employee's self-service portal declaration, no GL posting) â€” the two surfaces are never merged (see `docs/module-map.md`).
- **NoteFrais** (FG135) â€” an employee's out-of-pocket expense claim: `company`+`employe` FK, `categorie`, `montant` TTC, `motif`, `justificatif` photo (MinIO FileField), reference `NDF-YYYYMM-NNNN` (race-safe). Cycle `brouillonâ†’soumiseâ†’validee`(posts debit charge 6143 / credit personnel-crÃ©diteur 4432, **respecting the compta period lock**)`â†’remboursee`(debit 4432 / credit treasury GL) or `rejetee`. **XACC27** adds `hors_politique` (flagged when it beats a `PlafondNoteFrais.montant_max` for its `categorie` â€” warning only, never blocking). **XACC28** adds billable-expense refacturation fields (`refacturable`, `taux_marge`, opaque `client_refacturation_id`/`facture_refacturation_id` â€” string-refs, no ventes import). **ZACC6** adds an optional `rapport` FKâ†’`RapportNoteFrais` â€” once attached, the note is posted/reimbursed IN BLOCK by its report, never individually.
- **RapportNoteFrais** (ZACC6, Odoo Â« Expense Report Â» equivalent) â€” groups N `NoteFrais` of one employee into one submission: validation posts ONE aggregated Ã©criture (Î£ charges by compte / credit 4432), reimbursement settles in ONE payment. A note with `rapport=None` keeps its individual FG135 cycle untouched.
- **PlafondNoteFrais** (XACC27) â€” per-company, per-`NoteFrais.categorie` spend ceiling referential (`montant_max`, optional `seuil_justificatif_obligatoire`); a category with no configured row is never flagged. **NTP2P11** (migration frais/0003) adds `jours_max_apres_depense` + `escalade_direction_au_dela_de` (both optional, unset = no-op): at `soumettre_note_frais`, `services.note_frais_warning_delai`/`note_frais_escalade_direction` stamp `NoteFrais.warning_delai` (non-blocking text) / `escalade_direction` (bool) server-side and journal them to `records.Activity` (best-effort, never blocks the submission). When `stock.AchatsParametres.sod_stricte` is on, the note's own creator cannot validate its DIRECTION escalation (`_verifier_separation_taches_note_frais`, reads `stock.selectors` only).
- **BaremeIndemnite** + **IndemniteChantier** (FG136) â€” kilomÃ©trique/per-diem site-trip allowances: `BaremeIndemnite` (per-company `taux_km` MAD/km + `per_diem` MAD/jour, one `defaut` active barÃ¨me); `IndemniteChantier` computes `distance_km` via a LOCAL haversine copy (keeps `frais` decoupled from installations/sav) between GPS dÃ©part/chantier Ã— `taux_km` Ã— (`aller_retour` ? 2 : 1) + `per_diem` Ã— `nombre_jours`, all FROZEN at calc. Same `brouillonâ†’soumiseâ†’validee`(posts)`â†’remboursee`/`rejetee` cycle as NoteFrais, respecting the period lock.
- Endpoints (dual-served, byte-identical, at BOTH `/api/django/frais/â€¦` and the legacy `/api/django/compta/â€¦`): `notes-frais/` (+ `soumettre|valider|rejeter|rembourser`), `rapports-notes-frais/`, `plafonds-notes-frais/`, `baremes-indemnite/` (auto-demotes prior default), `indemnites-chantier/` (+ lifecycle actions). **NTP2P26/27** add `notes-frais/releve/` â€” a per-period statement of expense notes (PDF via `pdf_notes_frais.py` or `?export=xlsx`).

### marketing â€” campaigns, sequences, events, loyalty, surveys (ODX9/10)  *( `/api/django/marketing/` )*
Previously undocumented here (36 models, predates this run). State-only split OUT of `apps.compta` (**ODX9**, same `db_table='compta_<model>'`/`SeparateDatabaseAndState`/shim pattern as `frais`/`portail`); **ODX10** gave it its own `/api/django/marketing/â€¦` prefix while the historical `/api/django/compta/â€¦` routes keep serving the SAME ViewSets byte-identically (basenames prefixed `mkt-â€¦` to avoid router-name collisions). `apps.marketing.selectors` is the ONLY sanctioned read surface for other apps (never a `marketing.models` import) â€” **WIR96** (this run) is the first real cross-app consumer: `ouverture_partage_pour_token`/`relances_devis_abandonne`, read by `ventes.views.devis.suivi_partage` (see Â§4 ventes), and the public proposal open-hook now writes `OuverturePartage` (was routed but previously never populated).
- **Models, by sub-domain** â€” mailing (`Campagne`, `EnvoiCampagne` one row per recipient, `SuppressionMarketing` opt-out list, `ListeDiffusion`/`AbonnementListe`, `SegmentMarketing`, `RebondSoft`, `ApprobationEnvoiCampagne`, `DomaineEnvoi`); drip sequences (`SequenceRelance` triggered on a lead entering a funnel stage, `EtapeSequence`, `InscriptionSequence`, `ExecutionEtapeSequence`); link tracking (`LienTrackee`, `ClicLien`, `StatutEngagementContact`, **NTMKT18** `ScoreMaturite`/`VariationScoreMaturite` â€” multi-signal lead maturity score + its change history); quote recovery/capture (`RelanceDevisAbandonne`, `OuverturePartage`, `FormulaireIntake` + landing capture, `MessageWhatsAppEntrant`, `AppelTelephonique`); satisfaction (`EnqueteNPS`, `AvisClient`, `Enquete`/`ReponseEnquete` a configurable survey engine); loyalty (`CompteFidelite`, `MouvementFidelite`, `RegleUpsell`); events (`TypeEvenement`, `EvenementMarketing`, `BilletEvenement`, `QuestionEvenement`, `CommunicationEvenement`, `InscriptionEvenement`); plus `SupportOffline` (QR print collateral) and `PostSocial` (social content calendar).
- **Journey graph (NTMKT12/14/15)** â€” additive graph-routing layer over the existing linear `SequenceRelance`/`EtapeSequence` engine (a sequence with zero graph node still ticks exactly as before). `NoeudJourney` (dÃ©clencheur/attente(J+n)/**attente_jusqu_a** date-ou-crÃ©neau-ouvrÃ© (NTMKT14)/action/branche/sortie, free `config` JSON, `position_x/y` for the canvas) + `ArcJourney` (source/cible nÅ“uds, `condition` toujours/a_ouvert/a_clique/score_seuil/tag_present, `ordre` â€” supersedes `EtapeSequence`'s single condition with N evaluated arcs). `ModeleJourney` (NTMKT15, a ready-to-instantiate pre-built graph template, complementing the 5 linear XMKT20 recipes) â€” seeded by `manage.py seed_modeles_journey`. Endpoints `noeuds-journey/`, `arcs-journey/`, `modeles-journey/`; Celery beat `marketing.executer_journeys` (08:15, company-by-company).
- **Landing pages & content (NTMKT16/23/31)** â€” `VersionFormulaireIntake` (NTMKT16, an editorial version of a `FormulaireIntake` landing: titre/pitch/image, server-incremented `version`, `publie` flag â€” the public page always renders the latest published version). `BlocContenu` (NTMKT23, a reusable content fragment texte/image/cta; inserting COPIES it into the campaign body â€” editing the source never retro-edits a sent campaign). `ParametresMarketing` (NTMKT31, one singleton per company â€” expÃ©diteur nom/email/domaine, additive silence-window hours + daily send cap, all-NULL = unchanged behaviour). Endpoints `versions-formulaire-intake/`, `blocs-contenu/`, `parametres/` (NTMKT31).
- **Ops/reporting additions** â€” `heatmap-engagement/` (NTMKT24, read-only dayÃ—hour engagement heatmap), `campagnes/importer-couts/` (NTMKT26, CSV import of external Meta/Google Ads spend), `campagnes/{id}/rapport-pdf/` (NTMKT27, internal campaign-bilan PDF), `registre-consentement/export-pdf/` (NTMKT28, CNDP consent-register export), public `preferences/<token>/` (NTMKT22, self-service per-channel/per-list preference centre â€” never a blanket unsubscribe). `services.purger_tokens_expires` (NTMKT33, daily 03:00 beat, purges expired public unsubscribe/preference tokens) and `services.rappeler_approbations_envoi_en_attente` (NTMKT35, 4-hourly beat, one reminder per pending campaign-send approval past 24h â€” anti-duplicate).
- Endpoints (dual-served at both `/api/django/marketing/â€¦` and the legacy `/api/django/compta/â€¦`): `campagnes/`, `envois-campagne/`, `approbations-envoi-campagne/`, `listes-diffusion/`, `abonnements-liste/`, `segments-marketing/`, `sequences-relance/`, `etapes-sequence/`, `inscriptions-sequence/`, `relances-devis-abandonnes/`, `ouvertures-partage/`, `formulaires-intake/`, `messages-whatsapp/`, `appels/`, `enquetes-nps/`, `avis-clients/`, `comptes-fidelite/`, `mouvements-fidelite/`, `regles-upsell/`, `enquetes/`, `evenements-marketing/`, `inscriptions-evenement/`, `types-evenement/`, `billets-evenement/`, `questions-evenement/`, `communications-evenement/`, `supports-offline/`, `domaines-envoi/`; **NTMKT18-20/39-41** add `scores-maturite/<lead_id>/` (current maturity score + history), `attribution/comparaison/` (the 4 attribution models side-by-side for a signed devis), `campagnes/export/` (XLSX), `campagnes/<pk>/envois/export/` (CSV), `segments-marketing/<pk>/export/` (XLSX of resolved members), `evenements-marketing/<pk>/importer-inscrits/` (bulk CSV/XLSX import); public token/slug views `webhooks/brevo/`, `webhooks/sms-stop/`, `desinscription/<token>/`, `preferences/<token>/`, `double-optin/<token>/`, `r/<token>/` (tracked-link redirect), `enquetes-publiques/<token>/(+soumettre)`, `evenements-marketing/<id>/inscription-publique/`, `intake/<slug>/(+soumettre)`. **PACT106/107/108** give `avis-clients/` (FG239, + Google Reviews routing), `enquetes-nps/` (FG238, distinct from the Pulse eNPS survey) and `messages-whatsapp/` (FG207, read-only inbound log) their first screens (`/marketing/avis-clients`, `/marketing/enquetes-nps`, `/marketing/messages-whatsapp`). Frontend: `JourneyBuilder`/`LandingVersions`/`HeatmapEnvoi`/`UtmBuilder` (NTMKT25, campaign URL/UTM generator, no model) screens + guided `CampagneWizard`/`SequenceWizard`/`EnqueteWizard` (NTMKT29/30/32, opened from their list's Â« (guidÃ©) Â» button) at `/marketing/{campagnes,sequences,enquetes}/nouveau` + `/marketing/parametres` (`MarketingParametres.jsx`) + `/marketing/generateur-url`. **NTMKT44/45** add `CampagneViewSetAudite`/`EnqueteNPSViewSetNotifiant` + `evenement_inscription_publique_notifiante` â€” envelope subclasses/wrappers over the `apps.compta` originals (audit-log the real send / notify on an NPS detractor / notify on public event signup) registered ONLY on the marketing router; the legacy `/compta/â€¦` routes keep serving the unmodified originals.

### flotte â€” fleet: vehicles + rolling equipment (FLOTTE1, new app)  *( `/api/django/flotte/` )*
- **Vehicule** (`company` FK; immatriculation, marque, modÃ¨le, Ã©nergie diesel/essence/Ã©lectrique/hybride, kilomÃ©trage, valeur, statut actif/maintenance/rÃ©formÃ©) and **EnginRoulant** (`company` FK; type nacelle/groupe Ã©lectrogÃ¨ne/chariot, compteur d'heures, marque, modÃ¨le, valeur, statut). Company-scoped ViewSets (company forced server-side, an injected body `company` is ignored) at `vehicules/`, `engins/` with Ã©nergie/statut/type filters + search. Uses only the `authentication.Company` string FK â€” no domain-app imports. **FLOTTE3** adds `Vehicule.emplacement_stock_id` (PositiveInteger, NOT a cross-app FK) referencing a `stock.EmplacementStock`; validated same-company + labelled via a function-local `apps.stock.selectors.get_emplacement_scoped` call (degrades to `#id`; never imports stock models). **FLOTTE6** adds **ReferentielFlotte** (editable per-company lookup lists: `domaine` type_vehicule/type_engin/energie/categorie_permis, `code`/`libelle`/`ordre`/`actif`, unique company+domaine+code) â€” additive (hardcoded choices untouched) â€” plus an idempotent `seed_referentiels_flotte` command. ViewSet `referentiels/` (`?domaine`/`?actif`). **FLOTTE5** adds **ActifFlotte** â€” a unified asset reference linking entretien/sinistre/document to EITHER a `Vehicule` OR an `EnginRoulant` via one model (exactly-one-target + same-company enforced in `clean()`/`save()`); company-scoped ViewSet `actifs/` (`?type_actif`) + selectors for cross-app reads. **FLOTTE13** adds fuel/energy consumption: `selectors.consommation_vehicule` computes **L/100 km and kWh/100 km** plein-to-plein from `PleinCarburant` + odometer (per-segment, divide-by-zero guarded, L vs kWh kept separate), surfaced read-only at `pleins/consommation/?vehicule=<id>` (`IsAnyRole`, 400 missing/non-int param, 404 cross-company). No migration. **FLOTTE14** adds **CarteCarburant** (fuel card: `numero`, `plafond`, optional `vehicule`/`conducteur` FK) + `selectors.anomalies_pleins` flagging four families per plein â€” `km_recul` (odometer decreasing), `km_saut` (>5000 km jump), `conso_aberrante` (>2Ã— the vehicle's median baseline, reusing FLOTTE13's conso), `plafond_depasse` â€” at `cartes/anomalies/?vehicule=` (read action, `IsAnyRole`). ViewSet `cartes/`. **FLOTTE15** adds **PlanEntretien** (preventive maintenance via an `ActifFlotte` FK â€” reaches both Vehicule km and EnginRoulant heures): triggers by `intervalle_km`/`intervalle_jours`/`intervalle_heures` + last-done refs + alert margins; `selectors.plans_entretien_status` computes next-due vs current km/date/hours (due/upcoming/ok). ViewSet `plans-entretien/` (+ `echeances/` read action). **FLOTTE16** adds **EcheanceEntretien** (a generated due-maintenance record per plan: `due_le`/`due_km`/`due_heures`, `statut` a_faire/planifie/fait) + `services.generer_echeances_entretien` (idempotent â€” no duplicate OPEN Ã©chÃ©ance per plan; best-effort alert via `notifications.notify`) + a `manage.py generer_echeances_entretien` command. ViewSet `echeances-entretien/` (read any role, `generer` write action; POST-create disabled). Migration flotte/0014. **FLOTTE17** adds **Garage** (atelier: nom/adresse/tÃ©lÃ©phone) + **OrdreReparation** (repair order on an `ActifFlotte`: garage, description, `cout_main_oeuvre`+`cout_pieces`â†’derived `cout_total`, `statut` ouvert/en_cours/cloture, optional `EcheanceEntretien` link); ViewSets `garages/`, `ordres-reparation/` (+ `couts/` summary, `cloturer/` which solde the linked Ã©chÃ©ance). Migration flotte/0015. **FLOTTE18** adds **Pneumatique** (tire: position/dimension/montage/dÃ©pose/statut/coÃ»t) + **PieceFlotte** (part: dÃ©signation/rÃ©f/quantitÃ©/coÃ»t, optional OrdreReparation link); ViewSets `pneumatiques/`, `pieces/` + a per-vehicle `synthese/` summary. Migration flotte/0016. **FLOTTE19** adds **EcheanceReglementaire** â€” generic regulatory deadlines (visite technique/assurance/vignette/carte grise/taxe Ã  l'essieu) on an `ActifFlotte`, with a today-injectable status (a_jour/a_renouveler/expire) and `expirantes/?within=N`; distinct from the FLOTTE16 maintenance Ã©chÃ©ances. ViewSet `echeances-reglementaires/`. Migration flotte/0017. **FLOTTE20** adds **BaremeVignette** â€” an editable per-company TSAV grid (Ã©nergie Ã— CV bracket â†’ montant, per year) + `Vehicule.puissance_fiscale` + `selectors.calcul_tsav` (electric exempt, no bracket â†’ None) + an idempotent standard-grid seed. ViewSet `baremes-vignette/` + `vehicules/{id}/tsav/`.
